## 1. [MAYBE] UCSF | Research Data Analyst
- Link: https://iazuqy.fa.ocs.oraclecloud.com/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/en/sites/CX_1/job/11122
- Location: San Francisco, CA, United States US
- Posted: Nov 17 (board gives the date only)
- Scanner fit: ~80 | Min YOE: None | H-1B (certified LCAs, Oct 2024-Dec 2025): none found (check myvisajobs)
- Type: part-time (16 h/week stated)
- Title scope: core | Has: aws, dashboard, etl, postgres, r, regression, sql, statistic, cohort, data cleaning, git, healthcare, quantitative, reporting | Lacks: -  | Note: no H-1B filings found for this employer part-time, about 16 h/week: under the 20 h/week STEM OPT minimum
- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

This is a limited appointment position will end 12-months after the start date. Involves gathering, analyzing, and interpreting a wide variety of research data. Designs and conducts research including selecting data samples, developing research instruments, analyzing collected information according to established statistical methods, and developing recommendations based on research findings. Prepares reports, charts, tables, and other visual aids to interpret and communicate data and results. The UCSF Department of Anesthesia & Perioperative Care seeks a part-time (40%, 16 hours/week) Research Data Analyst to support the Transplant Anesthesia Research Group and the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database ( https://liverdatabase.com ), a multicenter research database integrating REDCap clinical forms, registry data, and anesthesia data from 16+ contributing transplant centers. Under the supervision of the Principal Investigator (PI), the incumbent performs defined duties supporting the Transplant Anesthesia Research Group and the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database, a multicenter observational research database. The position involves direct participation in data ingestion and validation, SQL and R programming, maintenance of an existing data application, and coordination of data with participating institutions. Ingest, validate, and reconcile site data submissions to the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database using the group's upload and validation application; review validation reports (missingness, outliers, out-of-codebook values, ID-label errors) and return audit sheets to sites. Run REDCap–registry data concordance checks; apply and document data corrections; and confirm the successful rebuild of the combined tables after each upload. Write SQL (PostgreSQL) and R code to extract cohorts, run QA queries, and produce summary tables and figures for research datasets, following the database's documented conventions. Maintain and extend the existing SATA data application (R/Shiny, PostgreSQL) using institution-approved AI coding tools (Claude Code): direct the tool, review generated changes, run the test suite against the local synthetic database, and commit through Git. Coordinate data follow-up with 16+ participating centers: track open items (data deliveries, audit-sheet returns, DUA status, IRB letters) in our CRM system and chase without prompting; support site onboarding and local merge and de-identification steps. Assist participating institutions with REDCap questions and local data management for the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database; consult the PI before contacting a site regarding missing or implausible data. Handle DUA-bound data transfers only through the approved secure file-sharing system; prepare IRB personnel and external-collaborator modifications for PI submission; complete UCSF-mandated compliance and privacy training. Post planned tasks at the start of each work day and a written end-of-day status (what changed, what is blocked, what is next); keep site-onboarding and data-handling SOPs and per-file data dictionaries current. Works on research data reporting assignments that are of moderate diversity in scope. Exercises judgment within generally defined practices and policies in selecting methods and techniques for obtaining solutions. Applies working proficiency in R and SQL to defined segments of an established multicenter observational database. Ingests, validates, and reconciles site data submissions; writes and maintains version-controlled extracts and reports; and maintains an existing data application using AI-assisted development tools, reviewing and testing generated changes before adoption. Coordinates data-related follow-up with participating institutions and keeps project documentation up to date. Every table, dataset, or figure is delivered to the research team with a written validation check (variable types, missingness, reconciled counts). The Research Data Analyst works under the direct supervision of Dr. Dieter Adelmann, co-principal investigator of the UCSF Transplant Anesthesia Research Group (TARG) and the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database. The position will comprise three main tasks: 1. To manage the onboarding of new sites: help each site set up its local REDCap project, execute a Data Use Agreement (DUA) with UCSF, link its local data to registry data, de-identify the data, and share it securely with UCSF. Working with the sites on data cleaning and outlier review, the analyst ensures that each dataset is complete, correctly linked, and consistent with the national registry (the Scientific Registry of Transplant Recipients, SRTR). 2. To help manage the ETL process for the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database: as part of the data coordinating center, the analyst will help maintain an existing R/Shiny upload tool that ingests data submitted by participating sites and loads it into a PostgreSQL database. The tool itself is maintained using Claude Code, which runs on AWS Bedrock for PHI compliance. 3. To build research datasets based on data from the SATA Liver Transplant Outcomes Database and our institutional data warehouse, the Transplant Outcomes and Anesthesia Database (TOAD). This role bridges data science and medicine and is well-suited to anyone seeking hands-on clinical research experience, including those preparing for a career in medicine or public health. We are looking for someone comfortable navigating databases, experienced with using AI coding agents responsibly, and fundamentally committed to strict compliance and data security when handling protected health information (PHI). Above all, you should bring a genuine curiosity for real-world clinical data. In addition to the technical work, the position offers close exposure to clinical data and to the clinical reasoning of our physician-researchers. How to apply: Submit a resume and a cover letter of no more than one page. Your cover letter must be written as exactly four numbered paragraphs in the order below, with a strict maximum of four sentences per paragraph. At the end of your letter, after paragraph (4), please explicitly acknowledge your understanding that this is a limited-time, 40% effort position requiring in-person work at the UCSF Parnassus campus; this acknowledgment does not count toward the four-paragraph or sentence limits. The four paragraphs: Summarize how your education and training so far make you the ideal candidate for this position, and how the experience gained in this position will help you reach your professional goals. Describe your practical experience working with large datasets, including the scale of the data and the tools you utilized. Detail your experience working/observing/volunteering in the medical field, or if you lack direct clinical experience, explain how your background prepares you to handle healthcare data. Describe a specific data or coding project you have worked on and explain exactly how that wo [...]

### Job description

Ready to make your next big professional move? Join us on our journey to achieve our big dream of building the most loved restaurant brands in the world. Restaurant Brands International Inc. is one of the world's largest quick service restaurant companies with nearly $49 billion in annual system-wide sales and over 33,000 restaurants in more than 120 countries and territories. RBI owns four of the world's most prominent and iconic quick service restaurant brands – TIM HORTONS®, BURGER KING®, POPEYES®, and FIREHOUSE SUBS®. These independently operated brands have been serving their respective guests, franchisees and communities for decades. Through its Restaurant Brands for Good framework, RBI is improving sustainable outcomes related to its food, the planet, and people and communities. RBI is committed to growing the TIM HORTONS®, BURGER KING®, POPEYES® and FIREHOUSE SUBS® brands by leveraging their respective core values, employee and franchisee relationships, and long track records of community support. Each brand benefits from the global scale and shared best practices that come from ownership by Restaurant Brands International Inc. Our opportunity: The Global Commodity Management & Analytics Specialist will support the Centre of Excellence (CoE) with commodity intelligence, data analysis, risk reporting and negotiation preparation. Based in Zug, Switzerland, the role will report to the Senior Manager, Global Commodity Management & Analytics. It will help develop analytical tools and processes that support the Procurement team in making informed commercial decisions across direct and indirect categories, including sugar, wheat, soy, corn, pork, beef, chicken, paperboard, potatoes, metals, steel, engineering, energy, MRO and industrial gases. RBI follows a 5 day, in-office work schedule to support collaboration. Candidates should be comfortable working onsite 5 days per week . Your roles & responsibilities: Support the development and maintenance of analytical tools, including forecasts, dashboards and scenario-planning templates. Check data quality, document assumptions and help prepare recurring reports. Collect and analyse market, supplier and commercial data across the portfolio. Identify relevant trends, check key assumptions and summarise findings for review by the team. Contribute to cost models and benchmarks by collecting, validating and analysing relevant data. Help compare supplier pricing and prepare clear insights for sourcing and commercial discussions. Support risk and scenario analysis by preparing data, checking assumptions and updating dashboards and reports. Support analytical and procurement projects by preparing cost analysis, market information and meeting materials. Coordinate timelines, maintain action trackers and support category leads with negotiation preparation. Assist with contingency and risk analysis by gathering inputs, updating scenario models and preparing reports. Coordinate information across teams, maintain action logs and escalate findings or data gaps to the relevant colleagues. Maintain analytical datasets and support external data sources and system integrations. Help keep information accurate, organised and accessible, and document data definitions and processes for procurement, finance and supply-chain teams. Coordinate project updates and prepare dashboards, reports and meeting materials for regional and global stakeholders. Track milestones and follow-up actions, and support senior colleagues with analysis and governance materials. Your skills and experience: 2-5 years of experience in analytics, procurement, supply chain or commercial finance. Previous experience in FMCG, food service, QSR or agri-food for raw materials or direct commodities is an advantage. Experience with digital transformation, data analytics and procurement systems is an advantage. Experience using analytical tools, forecasting methods or data models to support commercial decisions. Exposure to predictive analytics or machine learning is an advantage. Working knowledge of Excel and experience with at least one analytical or visualisation tool, such as Python, R, Power BI or Tableau. Willingness to develop skills in modelling and dashboarding. Understanding of commercial concepts such as pricing, cost drivers or risks. Experience using data sources, research platforms or business intelligence tools is an advantage. Previous exposure to scenario analysis, risk reporting or project coordination in a commercial or operational context is and advantage. Good analytical and project coordination skills, with the ability to work with data, identify trends, organise information and explain findings in a clear and structured way. Clear written and verbal communication skills. Able to prepare concise updates and present analysis to colleagues and stakeholders, with support as needed. Fluent English. Additional languages are an advantage. Benefits at all of our global offices are focused on physical, mental and financial wellness. We offer unique and progressive benefits, including a comprehensive global paid parental leave program that supports employees as they expand their families, free telemedicine and mental wellness support. Restaurant Brands International and all of its affiliated companies (collectively, RBI) are equal opportunity and affirmative action employers that do not discriminate on the basis of race, national origin, religion, age, color, sex, sexual orientation, gender identity, disability, or veteran status, or any other characteristic protected by local, state, provincial or federal laws, rules, or regulations. RBI's policy applies to all terms and conditions of employment. Accommodation is available for applicants with disabilities upon request.

### Job description

Newport, an Ascensus company, helps employers offer their associates a more secure financial future through retirement plans , insurance and consulting services . Newport offers comprehensive plan solutions and consulting expertise to plan sponsors and the advisors who serve them. As a provider and partner, Newport is independent, experienced, and responsive. The Reporting & KPI Analyst will play a key role in monitoring, analyzing, and reporting on the organization’s key performance indicators (KPIs) to support data-driven decision-making. This individual will be responsible for developing and maintaining performance dashboards, generating insights, and creating standardized reports to help stakeholders understand trends and measure progress against targets. This role ensures data integrity, timely reporting, and the accuracy of various performance indicators. The Reporting and KPI analyst will also collaborate with cross-functional teams to provide clear insights that support strategic decision-making. Section 2: Job Functions, Essential Duties and Responsibilities Data Collection & Analysis: Gather, process, and analyze data from various sources to produce insights on KPIs and performance metrics. KPI Monitoring: Track and report on KPIs regularly, highlighting variances from targets and identifying areas of opportunity or concern. Reporting & Dashboard Development: Create, maintain, and improve dashboards, scorecards, and visualizations that present KPIs and trends in a clear, actionable format. Collaborative Insights: Work closely with cross-functional teams (e.g., Consulting, Client Services, Accounting, Plan Administration, Operations) to understand their KPI needs and provide insights to guide strategic decisions. Data Quality Assurance: Ensure accuracy, consistency, and reliability of data by conducting regular audits and identifying potential data quality issues. Performance Evaluation: Analyze performance against benchmarks, forecasting future performance trends, and providing recommendations for improvement. Process Improvement: Identify and implement process improvements to streamline reporting, enhance data accuracy, and improve KPI tracking systems. Documentation & Training: Document reporting processes and provide training to users on accessing and interpreting reports. Responsible for protecting, securing, and proper handling of all confidential data held by Ascensus to ensure against unauthorized access, improper transmission, and/or unapproved disclosure of information that could result in harm to Ascensus or our clients. Our I-Client service philosophy and our Core Values of People Matter, Quality First and Integrity Always® should be visible in your actions on a day-to-day basis showing your support of our organizational culture. Supervision N/A Section 3: Experience, Skills, Knowledge Requirements Bachelor’s degree in business, Statistics, Data Analytics, Information Systems, or a related field. Strong understanding of KPI frameworks, data analytics, and business intelligence. Excellent analytical and problem-solving skills with strong attention to detail. Effective communication skills to present findings clearly to non-technical stakeholders. 2+ years of experience in a data analysis or reporting role. Proficiency in data visualization tools (e.g., Power BI, Tableau) and reporting tools (e.g., SQL, Excel). Knowledge of statistical analysis and data modeling is a plus. Experience in a similar industry or domain (e.g., finance, e-commerce, healthcare). Familiarity with ERP or CRM systems and data extraction processes. Experience with scripting or programming languages like Python or R. For all virtual remote positions, in order to ensure associates can effectively perform their job duties with no distractions, we require an uninterrupted virtual workspace and there is also an expectation of family care being in place during business hours. Additionally, there is an internet work speed requirement of 25 Mbps or better for individual use. If more than one person is utilizing the same internet connection in the same household or building, then a stronger connection is required. If you are unsure of your internet speed, please check with your service provider. Note: For call center roles specifically , it is a requirement to either hardwire your equipment directly to the internet router or ensure your workstation is in close proximity to the router. Please ensure that you are able to meet these expectations before applying. We are proud to be an

### Job description

Initial Posting Date: 11/16/2026 Application Deadline: 11/30/2026 Agency: Department of Justice Salary Range: $6,182 - $9,361 Position Type: Employee Position Title: Power BI Developer (Information Systems Specialist 6) Job Description: The Oregon Department of Justice (DOJ) , Information Services Section is seeking an experienced Power BI Developer (Information Systems Specialist 6) to join our team. This role supports the Department's Business Intelligence and Analytics program by designing, developing, enhancing, and supporting enterprise reporting and analytics solutions that empower business units to make informed decisions through secure, reliable, and accessible data products. The primary responsibility of this position is to develop and maintain Power BI reports, dashboards, semantic models, and related data solutions using Microsoft Power BI, Power Query, DAX, SQL, and approved data sources. This position works closely with business stakeholders, analysts, technical teams, and leadership to deliver trusted analytics solutions that improve operational insight, reduce manual reporting efforts, and support the Department's mission. This position may require on-call or after-hours support for the Origin batch process, as well as the ability to adapt across all functional coding areas when needed. Job Description Job Purpose The R&D Scientist for Data Analytics & Operations uses R&D-owned data and technical knowledge to deliver accurate, actionable analysis that supports business decisions across business areas such as carbon accounting, nutrient profiling and compliance reporting, including Extended Producer Responsibility (EPR). This position keeps that data accurate, consistent, traceable and accessible for use across enterprise systems and digital solutions through stewardship, governance and documentation, and helps prepare it for use in a variety of digital tools and solutions. Working with regular guidance alongside Procurement, Corporate ESG, digital and other partners, the role represents North America R&D in enterprise sustainability and compliance initiatives, serving as a trusted link between R&D subject-matter expertise and cross-functional teams. It suits data professionals with food or CPG experience as well as food or packaging scientists with strong data skills. Essential Functions & Responsibilities ​​Steward R&D data by investigating data-quality issues, reconciling discrepancies and keeping data accurate, consistent, and reliable across sources. ​Support data governance by applying established standards and maintaining definitions, mappings, taxonomy, metadata, validation checks and traceable documentation, flagging gaps to data owners ​Help prepare, structure and validate R&D data and content for ingestion into enterprise data platforms and downstream applications ​Query, combine and analyze R&D-owned product, ingredient, packaging and nutrition data to identify trends and deliver recurring reporting and analysis, for example carbon accounting, nutrient profiling and compliance reporting such as EPR ​Provide business requirements and validation criteria for automated, self-service and AI-enabled data solutions, and support testing for accuracy and relevance ​Use analytics to assess the impact of ingredient, packaging, specification or process changes across the portfolio by identifying affected products, technical dependencies and information gaps ​Participate in organizational sustainability and compliance initiatives, such as emissions-reduction and packaging programs, on behalf of North America R&D, contributing technical input and gathering information from R&D colleagues ​Collaborate with a wide range of cross-functional teams, support the use of relevant tools, data and guidance, and translate complex information for technical and non-technical partners ​ Manage multiple assignments across topic areas with regular guidance, moving between broad coverage and focused analysis as assignments require, prioritizing deliverables and escalating questions, risks and issues appropriately Expected Experience & Required Skills ​​Bachelor’s or Master’s degree in Food Science, Packaging, Chemical Engineering, Environmental Science, Agricultural Science, Nutrition, Data Science or a related field, or equivalent experience ​Minimum of two (2) years of relevant experience, or one (1) year with a Master’s degree, in data analysis, stewardship or reporting in a food, consumer packaged goods (CPG) or other regulated-product setting, or in food science, R&D or technical work with hands-on data analysis (post-graduation internship and co-op experience counts ). ​Proficiency in Excel and structured data analysis; SQL strongly preferred, or equivalent experience with tools such as Python or R. ​Working familiarity with R&D product, ingredient, packaging and nutrition data, and exposure to areas such as carbon accounting, nutrient profiling or regulatory reporting, including EPR. ​Learning agility, with a track record of quickly building expertise in new technical areas. ​Strong attention to detail and a commitment to data accuracy, validation and documentation. ​Effective communication, project management and problem-solving skills, including the ability to identify discrepancies, interpret findings and explain data findings to non-technical partners. ​Experience with Power BI or Power Platform, or familiarity with AI tools and with information and knowledge management concepts such as data governance, data stewardship, taxonomy and metadata. ​Ability to manage competing priorities and work collaboratively, with curiosity, sound judgment and a self-starter mindset. ​Willingness to travel up to 10%, with additional travel possible depending on location and business needs. ​ ​ Work Environment & Schedule We believe the office is a critical driver of innovation, connection, and development, but also realize providing flexibility to our employees is critical. To create a more agile and modern workplace, this position will be able to work virtually for up to two days each week and will be expected to work in-person at the assigned work location at least three days per workweek This job description is not designed to cover or contain all duties or responsibilities that are required of the role. Duties or responsibilities may change or be added with or without notice Our Total Rewards philosophy is to provide a meaningful and flexible spectrum of programs that equitably support our diverse workforce and their families and complement Kraft Heinz’ strategy and values. Kodiak AI, Inc. (Nasdaq: KDK), founded in 2018, is a leader in Physical AI, developing driverless technology that powers machines that move. Its Kodiak Driver is a vehicle-agnostic autonomous driving system that combines AI-powered software with modular hardware. Today it operates in long-haul trucking, industrial trucking, and defense. In 2024, Kodiak launched commercial Driver-as-a-Service operations with no one in the cab, and plans to launch driverless long-haul service between Dallas and Houston by the end of 2026. Kodiak has partnered with many of the biggest names in freight, including Bosch for scaled hardware production, AWS, and leading customers including Atlas Energy Solutions, IKEA, Bridgestone, Werner Enterprises, C.R. England, Roehl Transport, and General Dynamics Land Systems. We are looking for a Data Scientist with a background in data analysis, statistical modeling, data-centric research, and problem-solving. This person must understand the fundamentals of data interrogation frameworks, predictive analytics, and machine learning methods. The person in this role will be ensuring data cleanliness, eliminating statistical noise, and validating data metrics and dashboards to accelerate our engineering and commercial development. The role will work collaboratively across multiple teams to systematize data usage, analyze performance trends, and turn complex datasets into reliable, actionable insights. In this role, you will: Optimize and enhance existing metrics, dashboards, and data interrogation frameworks to support an efficient analytics process. Design and implement statistical and machine learning models to solve practical business problems and answer core product questions. Research and evaluate incoming datasets, distinguishing meaningful operational trends and conclusions from statistical noise. Facilitate the development of our data platform by ensuring data cleanliness, schema accuracy, and reliable underlying metrics. Collaborate with Software Engineers, Operations, and Autonomy teams to enable broad dissemination of data insights across the organization. Develop and maintain data visualization tools to ensure key findings are accessible and easily interpreted by all stakeholders. Serve as the domain expert across a broad range of data sources, monitoring real-time trends to maximize data utility over time. What you bring: Strong technical background as evidenced by a BS, MS, or PhD in Computer Science, Statistics, Operations Research, or a related quantitative field. 2+ years of academic or industry experience applying machine learning, mathematical optimization, and statistical modeling to practical business problems. Solid understanding of data aggregation, statistical interrogation metrics, and their underlying mathematical principles. Proficiency with Python, along with a willingness to learn C++. Experience with version control systems and modern software development workflows. Experience evaluating complex dataset releases (e.g., determining performance improvements in autonomy models vs. baseline). Familiarity with data platform infrastructure and ensuring dataset cleanliness. Bonus for experience in operations research or specialized quantitative modeling. Bonus for experience communicating insights to non-technical cross-functional teams. Bonus for experience in real-time data trend recognition and alerting. Great communicator; you must be capable of presenting clear, data-driven conclusions and writing concise documentation. The SouthState story is one of steady growth, deep community roots, and an unwavering commitment to helping our customers move forward. Since our beginnings in the 1930s to becoming a trusted financial partner across the South and beyond - we are known for combining personal relationships with forward-thinking solutions. We are committed to helping our team members find their success while maintaining the integrity of our values: building trust, fostering lasting relationships and pursuing excellence. At SouthState, individual contributions are recognized, potential is cultivated and team members are inspired to achieve their greater purpose. Your future begins here! Summary The Compensation Analytics & Technology Coordinator supports the Compensation Office by advancing data, analytics, technology, and process automation capabilities. This role connects Compensation, Human Resources, Technology, and business leaders to ensure compensation programs are supported by accurate data, scalable tools, efficient processes, and actionable insights. By helping modernize compensation administration through automation, dashboards, system integration, and AI-enabled decision support, this position strengthens governance and supports workforce planning, talent attraction, and retention. Duties & Responsibilities Develop, maintain, and enhance compensation dashboards, KPIs, executive reporting, and analytical tools to support compensation planning, workforce planning, labor cost analysis, salary movement, incentive effectiveness, pay equity, pay compression, and internal alignment reviews. Support compensation technology and systems by partnering with HRIS, Technology, vendors, and Compensation team members to optimize Workday functionality, improve system capabilities, support data integrations, and develop scalable self-service reporting tools. Design and improve technology-enabled processes for incentive administration, including eligibility tracking, goal administration, participant management, payout validation, automated reporting, controls, and audit-supporting documentation. Identify, evaluate, and support practical applications of AI, automation, and emerging technology to improve compensation decision-making, salary recommendations, market pricing, job evaluation, incentive analysis, and overall process efficiency. Establish and maintain compensation data governance practices, data quality controls, workflow documentation, and reporting standards to strengthen accuracy, transparency, auditability, and consistency across compensation and incentive processes. Support compensation strategy and future-state process design by contributing analytics and technology expertise to job architecture, salary structure, market pricing, compensation roadmaps, project implementations, and strategic workforce planning initiatives. It is the responsibility of this role to take ownership of all tasks and challenges that they encounter in the operation of their assigned position. Reasonable accommodations may be made to enable individuals with disabilities to perform the essential functions. Equivalent combination of education and directly related experience may be considered. Minimum Experience Three or more years of related experience in compensation, HR analytics, workforce analytics, HR technology, data reporting, or business analytics preferred. Experience supporting compensation planning, salary administration, incentive compensation, market pricing, reporting, dashboards, or process automation preferred. Experience working with HRIS, Workday, business intelligence tools, data management processes, or cross-functional project support preferred. Licenses & Certifications No specific license or certification required, however, CCP or PMP preferred. Compensation, HR analytics, Workday, data analytics, Power BI, Tableau, or related certification preferred. Additional Physical Demands None beyond the physical demands listed below. Knowledge, Skills, & Abilities Knowledge of compensation concepts, including salary administration, market pricing, incentive compensation, pay equity, pay compression, and internal alignment. Strong analytical skills with the ability to interpret data, identify trends, validate results, and translate findings into clear, actionable insights. Proficiency with Microsoft Excel and experience using reporting, dashboard, or business intelligence tools such as Power BI, Tableau, or similar platforms. Working knowledge of HRIS systems, compensation technology, data integrations, workflow documentation, and data quality practices; Workday experience preferred. Ability to support process automation, system enhancements, reporting improvements, and technology-enabled solutions that improve efficiency and governance. Familiarity with artificial intelligence, predictive analytics, machine learning concepts, or emerging technology applications that support compensation and workforce analytics preferred. Strong attention to detail, organizational skills, project coordination skills, and ability to manage multiple priorities while meeting deadlines. Ability to communicate technical information clearly to non-technical audiences and partner effectively with Compensation, Human Resources, Finance, Technology, vendors, and business leaders. Sound judgment, discretion, and ability to handle confidential compensation, employee, and business information appropriately. Physical Demands and Work Environment Physical Demands Ability to communicate in person, on the phone, and through electronic channels Ability to use a computer on a frequent basis, including typing and sustained attention to a monitor Ability to sit, walk, and/or stand for extended periods of time Ability to bend and reach Work Environment Office: If the position reports to a physical Company location, the setting will be a typical office environment. What You’ll Do: This role is the engine of our OTC daily pricing operations. Much of your day will be spent involved in detailed validation, investigating data anomalies, and communicating across teams. We are looking for someone who takes natural ownership of their work. The role requires proactive identification of pricing anomalies, independent price and curve verification, root-cause analysis and clear data-driven escalations to key stakeholders. Intellectual Curiosity : A natural drive to question how systems and models work, taking the initiative to test things on the side and learn underlying mechanics rather than blindly following a checklist. Proactive Ownership : A strong sense of initiative to independently and proactively resolve system issues or feed failures, deploying a practical workaround to keep pricing moving forward and suggesting a longer-term permanent solution. Technical Stack : Advanced Excel skills are mandatory. Familiarity with Python or VBA is needed but not required on day one. We realize that it's our people who give us a competitive advantage and drive success in the market, and our goal is to create an inclusive culture that rewards hard work. ### Job description

WHY FACULTY? 


We established Faculty in 2014 because we thought that AI would be the most important technology of our time. Since then, we’ve worked with over 350 global customers to transform their performance through human-centric AI. You can read about our real-world impact here https://faculty.ai/impact.



We don’t chase hype cycles. We innovate, build and deploy responsible AI which moves the needle - and we know a thing or two about doing it well. We bring an unparalleled depth of technical, product and delivery expertise to our clients who span government, finance, retail, energy, life sciences and defence.



Our business, and reputation, is growing fast and we’re always on the lookout for individuals who share our intellectual curiosity and desire to build a positive legacy through technology.



AI is an epoch-defining technology, join a company where you’ll be empowered to envision its most powerful applications, and to make them happen.



ABOUT THE TEAM



Our Energy, Transition and Environment business unit is pioneering meaningful change in the clean energy revolution. Our vision is to accelerate the transition to net-zero emissions and drive efficiencies for a new era of utility companies.

 

We believe that the responsible, and intelligent, deployment of AI is critical to the success of this mission. We partner with a wide range of clients - from major energy operators, to GreenTech startups, and national infrastructure providers - to build solutions which return measurable impact and move us towards a smarter, cleaner, and more sustainable world.

 



ABOUT THE ROLE:


As a Data Scientist II at Faculty, you will lead the design of end-to-end analytical approaches, developing high-quality models that solve complex, multi-faceted business problems. Joining our Applied AI division, you will leverage advanced programming to turn high-level client needs into rigorous mathematical frameworks. 

This role is for an ambitious practitioner ready to own the delivery of robust, scalable model components while ensuring every solution is mathematically sound and ethically responsible. You will play a vital part in pushing our technical boundaries and mentoring others.

 
 


WHAT YOU'LL BE DOING:


 - Designing and implementing core data science approaches for project workstreams, including the selection of optimal algorithms and validation strategies.

 - Combining deep domain expertise with advanced machine learning techniques to solve unique and complex business challenges.

 - Owning the end-to-end delivery of specific model components, ensuring they are scalable, robust, and mathematically precise.

 - Developing innovative algorithms or adapting existing frameworks to meet bespoke client requirements and technical constraints.

 - Building the software architecture required to wrap models and agentic workflows into production-grade, reliable environments.

 - Supporting Level 1 Data Scientists by providing technical guidance and conducting rigorous peer reviews on modeling tasks.

 - Managing personal delivery timelines while proactively identifying and mitigating technical risks or data limitations within projects.

 
 


WHO WE'RE LOOKING FOR:


 - You possess expertise in statistical modeling and deep learning frameworks, such as PyTorch or TensorFlow, to implement complex paradigms.

 - You are disciplined in extracting valid insights from messy or incomplete datasets, ensuring solutions remain robust under uncertainty.

 - You have the ability to translate high-level, ambiguous client needs into actionable data science requirements and mathematical frameworks.

 - You promote a culture of shared best practices, ensuring all AI development is transparent, ethical, and held to high peer-reviewed standards.

 - You are a clear communicator who can manage technical discussions with customers and articulate project feasibility and delivery timelines.

 - You are highly proficient in advanced Python and harness frameworks, capable of building the architectures necessary for production-grade AI.




OUR INTERVIEW PROCESS

 

 1. Talent Team Screen (30 minutes)

 2. Take Home Technical Assessment

 3. Technical Interview (90 minutes)

 4. Commercial Interview (60 minutes)










OUR RECRUITMENT ETHOS




We aim to grow the best team - not the most similar one. We know that diversity of individuals fosters diversity of thought, and that strengthens our principle of seeking truth. And we know from experience that diverse teams deliver better work, relevant to the world in which we live. We’re united by a deep intellectual curiosity and desire to use our abilities for measurable positive impact. We strongly encourage applications from people of all backgrounds, ethnicities, genders, religions and sexual orientations.



If you don’t feel you meet all the requirements, but are excited by the role and know you bring some key strengths, please don't hesitate in applying as you might be right for this role, or other roles. We are open to conversations about part-time hours. 



A note on AI: we're happy for you to use it for research and interview prep, but please don't use it to generate answers during live interviews. We also use an AI note-taker (Metaview) in interviews so interviewers can stay present (which you can opt out of just let us know,) and every application is reviewed by a human, never decided by AI.

Job Summary: Deutsche Asset Management is seeking a Structured Finance Analyst to support the research, analysis, and ongoing surveillance of commercial mortgage-backed securities (CMBS) and residential mortgage-backed securities (RMBS). This position is in the Structured Finance Team, a key part of Deutsche Asset Management's Americas investment division. The team covers a wide range of products, including open-end mutual funds, institutional and retail separately managed accounts. The Structured Finance Team is seeking a candidate to join the team based in New York. Your Key Responsibilities: The candidate will assist team members in evaluating both new issue transactions and surveilling existing portfolio holdings across RMBS and CMBS Formulate high conviction investment recommendations based on relative value, factoring in various account risk tolerances Reviewing offering memoranda (interpreting interpret legal and structural provisions) Underwriting collateral (including property-level cash flows, DSCR, LTV, and sponsor quality), Building and maintaining cash flow models to assess structure and credit. Lead efforts to maintain data integrity within various portfolio management and trading systems Your Skills and Experience: Preferred candidates will have prior experience at a rating agency or buy-side investment firm with proven expertise in Mortgage Backed Securities (MBS) including CMBS and/or RMBS Bachelor’s degree Highly proficient with Excel and industry-standard tools such as Intex, Trepp, and Bloomberg Have a keen sense of integrity and fiduciary responsibility; be client focused and a strong team player Strong written and oral communication skills. Proven ability to leverage AI tools to enhance productivity, optimize workflows to solve business problems, while applying critical judgment to ensure responsible and ethical use of data and AI outputs. What we’ll offer you: At Deutsche Asset Management, we’re serious about diversity, equity and creating an inclusive culture where colleagues can be themselves sand it’s important to us that you enjoy coming to work - feeling healthy, happy and rewarded. At Deutsche Asset Management , you’ll have access to a range of benefits which you can choose from to create a personalized plan unique to your lifestyle. Whether you’re interested in healthcare, company perks, or are thinking about your retirement plan, there’s something for everyone. The salary range for this position in New York is $133,000 to $206,000. Actual salaries may be based on a number of factors including, but not limited to, a candidate’s skill set, experience, education, work location and other qualifications. Posted salary ranges do not include incentive compensation or any other type of renumeration. At Deutsche Asset Management , we recognize that our benefit programs have a profound impact on our colleagues. That’s why we are focused on providing benefits and perks that enable our colleagues to live authenti­cally and be their whole selves, at every stage of life. We provide access to physical, emotional, and financial wellness benefits that allow our colleagues to stay financially secure and strike balance between work and home. Click here to learn more! Deutsche Asset Management's Hybrid Working model is designed to find the right balance between in-person collaboration and engagement in the office, which is core to our working culture, and working from home. Each business group varies on the split between time spent in the office and at home, but this will be discussed and made clear as part of your application and interview process. We will continue to review and evolve our working environments and methods to ensure that we are working in the best way possible for our people. If you require any adjustments or changes to be made to the interview process for any reason including, or related to a disability or long-term health condition, then please contact your recruiter and let them know what assistance you may need. ### Job description

The mission of the Bureau of Environmental Disease Prevention is to prevent environmental disease in homes, communities, and the workplace, and to protect health by promoting healthy environments and health equity. The OEI Research Assistant will answer OEI’s main complaint line, relaying messages to appropriate office/field staff for follow-up. Under supervision, with latitude for independent judgement, will perform complex research and clerical tasks, and will report and assist the program’s senior administrative lead as necessary. DUTIES WILL INCLUDE BUT NOT BE LIMITED TO: - Data entry - Review and verify previously entered data for accuracy, validity, and that it complies with set protocols - Answer OEI’s main line and relay messages to the relevant personnel - Serve as a liaison between clients and office/field personnel on the matter of incoming complaints - Prepare written reports on weekly field activities - Maintain accurate digital archiving of all forms and reports - Assist with mail delivery/pick-up from building’s central mailroom - Other duties as assigned PREFERRED SKILLS: Previous experience researching information online, a strong customer service background, excellent oral and written communication skills, courteous telephone manner, basic computer literacy with the desire to learn additional computer skills Why you should work for us: - Loan Forgiveness: As a prospective employee of the City of New York, you may be eligible for federal/state loan forgiveness and repayment assistance programs that lessen your payments or even fully forgive your full balance. For more information, please visit the U.S. Department of Education’s website (https://studentaid.gov/pslf/) - Benefits: City employees are entitled to unmatched benefits such as: o a premium-free health insurance plan that saves employees over $10K annually, per a 2024 assessment. o additional health, fitness, and financial benefits may be available based on the position’s associated union/benefit fund. o a public sector defined benefit pension plan with steady monthly payments in retirement. o a tax-deferred savings program and o a robust Worksite Wellness Program that offers resources and opportunities to keep you healthy while serving New Yorkers. - Work From Home Policy: Depending on your position, you may be able to work up to two days during the week from home. - Job Security - you could enjoy more job security compared to private sector employment and be able to contribute to making NYC a healthy place to live and work. Established in 1805, the New York City Department of Health and Mental Hygiene (NYC Health Department) is the oldest and largest health department in the U.S., dedicated to protecting and improving the health of NYC. Our mission is to safeguard the health of every resident and cultivate a city where everyone, regardless of age, background, or location, can achieve their optimal health. We provide a wide array of programs and services focused on food and nutrition, anti-tobacco support, chronic disease prevention, HIV/AIDS treatment, family and child health, environmental health, mental health, and social justice initiatives. As the primary population health strategist and policy authority for NYC, with a rich history of public health initiatives and scientific advancements, from addressing the 1822 yellow fever outbreak to the COVID-19 pandemic, we serve as a global leader in public health innovation and expertise. Come join us and help to continue our efforts in making a difference in the lives of all New Yorkers! Commitment to Equity: The City of New York is an inclusive

Salary starting from £46k, salary dependant on skills and experience Permanent Full time / 37 hours a week Hybrid role, working from Huntingdon or Lincoln 26 day annual leave, annual bonus, double match pension (up to 7%), private healthcare Anglian Water offers a flexible approach; this role provides you the flexibility to work from home and from an Anglian Water office. Join Us as a Data Engineer – Shape the Future of the Datacore at Anglian Water We’re on the lookout for a passionate and experienced Data Engineer to join our dynamic and fast-growing team. This is a fantastic opportunity to play a key role in managing, optimising, and evolving the Datacore Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) platform — enabling the delivery of critical Data products that power our business. In this hands-on role, you’ll be responsible for the day-to-day technical operations of the Datacore — implementing, maintaining, and enhancing the data solutions for our OT business needs. Your key responsibilities will include: Delivering Data solutions for our Operational technology (OT) needs which will include creating ingestion pipelines, applying data cleansing rules and integrating with various systems Delivering and configuring data-focused application changes Monitoring performance of the data-focused applications you are charged with Ensuring our data products meet service commitments and provide automated performance insights Troubleshooting and preventing operational issues Automating provisioning, testing, and deployment processes Maintaining and enforcing best-practice operational procedures Leading and contributing to Communities of Practice to drive continuous improvement and innovation As a valued employee, you’ll be entitled to: Full private healthcare with no excess 26 days leave, rising with service + Bank Holidays, with the option to swap Christmas and Easter holidays for those celebrated by your religion A flexible working culture Competitive pension scheme – we double-match your contributions up to 7% Life Assurance at eight times your salary Personal Accident cover – up to 5x your salary Bonus Scheme Lots of great discounts Flexible benefits to support your wellbeing and lifestyle What does it take to be a Data Engineer? We’re looking for someone who truly embodies our values — doing the right thing, building trust, and always exploring new ways to improve. You’ll lead by example, fostering collaboration and inspiring those around you. You'll also: Strong experience in data engineering Experience/understanding of Data Product lifecycle management Strong analytical and problem solving skills Ability to make recommendations and take decisions Highly organised with effective time management and planning skills Excellent attention to detail Willingness to adapt to change Enjoys technical challenges and learning new skills Take ownership of risks and support robust risk management Champion knowledge sharing and operational excellence Support our Digital Engineering Centre for Enablement and Communities of Practice Key Skills and experience: Highly proficient in Python and SQL Proficient in Datacore application stack Proficient in technologies like Bicep, Python, Docker, Kubernetes, Azure Kubernetes Service (AKS) Proficient in Data Pipelines (both low code and Azure) Experience in configuring and maintaining IIoT applications such as TimeSeries Databases or Industrial Data Ops Tools Understanding of Agile delivery frameworks and methodologies Working Knowledge of Service Management processes, specifically Change, Incident, Event, Configuration and Problem Management Why Anglian Water? Anglian Water is not your typical water company. What we do really matters. Water is the lifeblood of our world and we’re proud of the difference we make. We put people at the heart of our business, and we truly love what we do! If you’re passionate about what you do and would like to make a difference, then we’d love to hear from you. Closing date: 1st December 2026 #loveeverydrop #LI-ZP1

### Job description

The LGIMA Investment Analytics team is responsible for providing market-leading analytics for the firm’s suite of investment solutions. This mandate encompasses areas such as performance, attribution, risk analytics and benchmark construction for the benefit of our clients and internal portfolio management teams, in addition to building tools to support analysis. The team partners with Client Solutions, Portfolio Management, Marketing, Operations, Data Management and IT to deliver on our goals. Our focus on scalability, quality and sophistication of analytics is essential to supporting LGIMA’s objective of providing a superior client experience. This role will be responsible for the delivery of performance and portfolio analytics that serves our internal and external clients across LGIMA’s credit, index, LDI and multi-asset investment strategies. This role will also involve working with 3rd party analytics platforms and enhancing the processes in place around these systems. As the individual grows into the role, more emphasis will be placed on building processes and tools to enhance scalability, allowing for increased contribution in a project-based and proactive analysis capacity. Own the production process for performance, attribution and portfolio analytics reporting. Fulfill portfolio analytics and data requests from internal teams, including Client Solutions, Marketing and Portfolio Management. Develop understanding of investment strategies, identifying anomalies in attribution and drivers of performance. Analyze and interpret portfolio risk characteristics and attribution factors to communicate the investment process and execution. Develop subject matter expertise in client strategies and operational aspects of the firm in order to lead related projects. Partner with Operations, IT and Data Management to enhance analytics system accuracy, capabilities and scalability. Over time, identify opportunities to enhance scalability through automated processes. Create custom portfolio analytics views in partnership with Client Solutions, Portfolio Managers and Marketing for client meetings and internal analysis. Undergraduate degree in a quantitative field, such as Finance, Economics, Computer Science, Mathematics or Engineering is desired. Progress towards or an interest to pursue an advanced degree and/or CFA is a plus. Strong quantitative, analytical and problem solving skills along with a high attention to detail and accuracy. 3-5 years of related performance measurement, attribution and/or risk analysis experience. Solid understanding of capital markets and the investment management business. Knowledge of fixed income and derivatives (options, swaps, futures, etc.) Intuitive and intellectually curious; ability to identify themes within data sets Demonstrated organizational and project management skills; ability to multi-task and to manage complex assignments. Self-confidence; ability to function as both a self-starter and a team player. Excellent verbal and written communications skills, with the ability to interact with all levels of LGIMA management as well as colleagues in global offices. Highly proactive and energetic focus toward internal and external client service; ability to consistently meet all internal/external deadlines Strong individual initiative with the ability to take a proactive approach to assigned projects or team goals. Flexibility; ability to adapt to growth and change Intermediate coding skillset (advanced Excel/VBA, SQL, etc.) or desire to learn. Experience with market data and portfolio analytics tools, such as Bloomberg, FactSet, etc. desired. The starting salary offer will vary based on multiple factors, including but not limited to the applicant’s education, job-related experience, skills, and abilities, geographic location, and market factors. This position is also eligible to participate in the company’s annual discretionary bonus plan. Full time employees may be eligible for health insurance with an optional HSA, short term disability, long term disability, dental insurance, vision care, life insurance, Healthcare, Dependent and Limited Care Flexible Spending Accounts, 401K, vacation, sick time, an employee assistance program, and commuter and transit programs. Additional voluntary programs include: supplemental health benefits including accidental injury, critical illness and hospital indemnity insurance and pet insurance. Job Title Research Assistant Agency Texas A&M University Department Psychology Proposed Minimum Salary Commensurate Job Location College Station, Texas Job Type Staff Job Description Here's a Glimpse of the Job The Research Assistant will support and coordinate research projects within Dr. MacNamara’s Multimethod Affect and Cognition Lab (MAClab) in the Department of Psychology. The selected candidate will contribute to research examining emotion and cognition in health and psychiatric conditions, particularly anxiety and depression, while supporting treatment-related research. Opportunities to Contribute Collects psychophysiological and functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data from human research participants. Supervises and supports undergraduate research assistants in data collection procedures. Conducts phone screenings and interviews with participants experiencing anxiety and/or depression. Provides administrative tasks related to grants and projects in the lab, including but not limited to preparation of IRBs and reports to funders. Recruits and screens potential research participants to determine eligibility. Assists with EEG and fMRI research studies and related data collection procedures. Prepares IRB applications, grant documents, and other research materials. Assists with additional research and laboratory tasks as needed. Qualifications Bachelor’s degree in Psychology, Neuroscience, Biology, or equivalent combination of education and experience. Ability to travel independently in employee-owned vehicle to MRI scanner on campus (10-minute drive from main campus). A well-qualified candidate for this position will also possess: Prior experience working in psychology, psychiatry (or related field) research lab. Prior experience with emotion-focused psychophysiology research. Master’s degree in Psychology, Neuroscience, Biology, or related field (completed by Fall 2026) or equivalent combination of education and experience. Statistical expertise. Ability to work independently, multitask and work cooperatively with others. Strong organizational skills. Ability to effectively communicate both orally and in writing. Ability to positively and professionally interact with research participants and other lab members. Working knowledge of office software; strong computer skills. Salary: $45,000 - $50,000 Compensation will be commensurate to selected hire’s experience. Special Note: This position is funded by grant and/or contract funding, which is renewed through provisions of the grantor of the contract. Therefore, continuation of this position will be reviewed annually and is contingent upon continuation of funding from these grants and/or contracts, as well as satisfactory job performance. Why Texas A&M University? Texas A&M University is committed to enriching the learning and working environment by promoting a culture that respects all perspectives, talents & lived experiences. Embracing varying opinions and perspectives strengthens our core values which are: Respect, Excellence, Leadership, Loyalty, Integrity, and Selfless Service. The Department of Psychological and Brain Sciences is engaged in research that falls into all five graduate areas of specialization: Behavioral and Cellular Neuroscience Clinical Psychology Cognition and Cognitive Neuroscience Psychology Industrial/Organizational Psychology Social & Personality Psychology In addition to the Department of Psychological and Brain Science’s 5 areas of specialization, we have overlapping research interests that bridge traditional areas of psychology. These primary research themes include Affective Science; Neuroscience; Personality and Individual Differences. To learn more please visit our site at https://artsci.tamu.edu/psychological-brain-sciences/index.html We are a prestigious university with strong traditions, Core Values, and a community of caring and collaboration. Amenities associated with a major university, such as sporting and cultural events, state-of-the-art recreation facilities, the Bush Library and Museum, and much more await you. Experience all that a big city has to offer but with a reasonable cost-of-living and no long commutes. Medical, prescription drug, dental, vision, life and AD&D, flexible spending accounts , and long-term disability insurance with Texas A&M contributing to employee health and basic life premiums. 12-15 days of annual paid holidays. Up to eight hours of paid sick leave and at least eight hours of paid vacation each month. Automatic enrollment in the Teacher Retirement System of Texas Health and Wellness: Free exercise programs and release time Professional Development: All employees have access to free LinkedIn Learning training, webinars, and limited financial support to attend conferences, workshops, and more. Educational release time and tuition assistance for completing a degree while a Texas A&M employee. Living Well, a program at Texas A&M that has been built by employees, for employees. Instructions to Applicants: Applications received by Texas A&M University must either have all job application data entered, or a resume attached. Failure to provide all job application data or a complete resume could result in an invalid submission and a rejected application. We encourage all applicants to upload a resume or use a LinkedIn profile to prepopulate the online application. All positions are security-sensitive. Applicants are subject to a criminal history investigation, and employment is contingent upon the institution’s verification of credentials and/or other information required by the institution’s procedures, including the completion of the criminal history check. Equal Opportunity/Veterans/Disability Employer.

SentiLink https://www.sentilink.com/ helps institutions and protects customers by accurately resolving identities and flagging fraud risk. Each day, we verify more than 3 million identities, flagging 150k+ fraud attempts in the process. The problems we work on are challenging, and solving them takes deep domain expertise, rigorous analysis, and a willingness to dig into the details.

At SentiLink, you'll work alongside smart, highly collaborative colleagues who respect each other's time and judgment, take ownership, and follow through on their commitments. Together, we do truly meaningful work protecting the identities of innocent consumers and flagging the fraudsters attempting to exploit them.

We're well capitalized and growing quickly. We already serve 13 of the top 15 U.S. banks, we're expanding into new markets, and we're backed by Craft Ventures, Andreessen Horowitz, NYCA, and Max Levchin. We've been named to the Forbes Fintech 50 https://www.forbes.com/companies/sentilink/?list=fintech50 and as a 2026 FICO Industry Vanguard Decision Award Winner https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260330439998/en/SentiLink-Named-a-2026-FICO-Industry-Vanguard-Decision-Award-Winner-for-Leadership-in-Identity-Theft-Detection. We were also the first company to go live https://resources.sentilink.com/blog/sentilink-makes-history-as-first-ecbsv-provider with the electronic Consent Based Social Security Number Verification (eCBSV) service, and we've testified before the U.S. Congress https://resources.sentilink.com/media/dr.-david-maimons-congressional-testimony-on-emerging-fraud-threats-and-the-evolving-fraud-landscape on the future of identity.

We want SentiLink to be the best place you've ever worked. We offer generous benefits and support a range of working arrangements, from fully remote to in-office. We are a digital-first company with strong collaboration across the U.S. and India, and we meet in person regularly to build relationships. We have offices in Austin, San Francisco, New York City, Seattle (Bellevue), Los Angeles, and Chicago in the U.S., and in Gurugram (Delhi) and Bengaluru in India.


ROLE:

SentiLink earns trust with prospects and customers (“partners”) by quantifying value with clear, data-backed business cases. The Solution Analytics team turns pilot datasets into rigorous retrospectives and ROI narratives that inform partner decisions and accelerate adoption. We also support existing partners with targeted, high-impact analyses that surface opportunities, de-risk implementations, and demonstrate ongoing value.

We’re looking for an analyst who blends business acumen with analytical rigor—someone who can frame ambiguous questions, structure analyses, and distill complex findings into executive-ready stories. You have a track record of translating data into decisions, building compelling presentations, and delivering them to senior stakeholders. Success in this role means owning the end-to-end analytics workflow (from scoping and methodology through insights and recommendations), communicating crisply, and influencing outcomes across Sales, Partner Success, Product, and Engineering.



This is a remote, US-based role.




RESPONSIBILITIES:

 - Conduct client discovery calls with account executives.

 - Meet with clients to discuss fraud strategies to mitigate risk.

 - Strategizing with the sales team to ensure alignment with partner’s needs.

 - Create and deliver presentations aligned to proposed solution value.

 - Support the sales team with all technical related queries.

 - Establish best practices across all solution analytics team members through effective coaching.

 - Design and execute analysis for white papers and other industry materials. Identity relevant insights, e.g., based on emerging fraud tactics, and collaborate on publishing

 - Develop relationships and maintain customer relationships.


REQUIREMENTS:

 - 3-5 years work experience at a VC-backed startup, management consulting, banking or private equity firm with an analytical focus

 - Strong analytical & communication skills, comfortable with ambiguity, a quick learner, drives work independently

 - Enthusiastic about becoming an expert in topics related to the future of financial services, fraud & identity

 - Exceptional interpersonal skills

 - Ability to present ideas in a clear, user-friendly way

 - Technical skills: you should be a pro with Google docs, sheets & slides; proficiency with SQL and/or Python a plus

 - Bachelor’s degree in computer science or software engineering (ability to write scripts), a plus

 - Candidates must be legally authorized to work in the United States and must live in the United States


SALARY RANGE:

 - $160,000/year - $190,000/year + equity + benefits


PERKS:

 - Employer paid group health insurance for you and your dependents

 - 401(k) plan with employer match (or equivalent for non US-based roles)

 - Flexible paid time off

 - Regular company-wide in-person events

 - Home office stipend, and more!




CORPORATE VALUES:

 - Follow Through

 - Deep Understanding

 - Whatever It Takes

 - Do Something Smart

If you want to work on hard problems with real-world impact, join us. &nbsp; About the Role We are seeking a full-time AI Researcher - New Grad (PhD) to join our founding team and bridge the gap between high-level AI research and real-world physical actuation for our next-generation autonomous transport platforms. In this role, you will lead and drive core research initiatives across Perception, World Models, and Action models. You will translate cutting-edge theoretical breakthroughs into scalable, real-world deployment on physical autonomous systems, shaping how our platforms perceive, simulate, and navigate complex environments. &nbsp; What you'll do Lead and integrate emerging research from the broader AI community, identifying, prototyping, and deploying the most promising solutions. Design, train, and deploy end-to-end multimodal models that translate real-time visual perception and high-level behavioral goals into physical vehicle actuation. Research, architect, and develop cutting-edge reinforcement learning and distillation techniques for trajectory planning. Develop interactive world models from raw multi-sensor logs, allowing the team to re-simulate events and query what a vehicle would see if it altered its trajectory. Ensure core autonomous driving models can seamlessly adapt to novel urban environments and rare edge cases. Partner with validation, systems, and engineering teams to benchmark models through rigorous simulated and on-vehicle scenarios, detecting regressions and resolving systemic performance bottlenecks. &nbsp; What we're looking for Education: PhD degree in Computer Science, EECS, Robotics, AI, or a closely related field (completed or anticipated completion within the current academic year). Research Track Record: Demonstrated publication record (including first-author papers or accepted work) in top-tier AI or robotics conferences (e.g., NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, CVPR, ICCV, ECCV, RSS, ICRA, CoRL). Core Expertise: Deep doctoral research focus in at least one of the following domains: Generative World Models, Video Generation, or Diffusion Models Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models or embodied AI Reinforcement Learning (RL), trajectory optimization, or decision-making systems Large-scale multimodal Transformer architectures applied to robotics or spatio-temporal data Framework Fluency: Advanced hands-on proficiency with PyTorch or JAX for training, distributed scaling, and fine-tuning large foundation models. Programming: Strong production-level proficiency in Python and working knowledge of C++ for performant system integration. Preferred qualifications Prior research or industry internship experience in autonomous driving, mobile robotics, or spatial AI. Hands-on experience with multi-modal sensor fusion across camera rigs, LiDAR, and Radar. Familiarity with Bird’s-Eye-View (BEV) representations, 3D occupancy forecasting, multi-task learning, or specialized spatial tokenization strategies. Practical experience with large-scale distributed training on GPU/TPU clusters using frameworks such as DeepSpeed, Megatron-LM, Ray, or PyTorch FSDP. &nbsp; Why join us At Atoms, you’ll work on one of the defining challenges of our time bringing automation into the physical world to drive real, lasting impact. We exist to uncover valuable unknown truths and turn them into progress, which means constantly pushing beyond what’s known and building what doesn’t yet exist. The work is ambitious and often challenging, but it’s grounded in a shared sense of purpose and a team committed to seeing it through together. Our work only matters if it serves others, and we know that meaningful progress depends on the trust of the people we serve and the strength of our team so we invest in both, creating an environment where you can do your best work and grow. &nbsp; What else you need to know This role is based in our Mountain View or San Francisco office. Atoms is a company driven by invention and continuous change, we are constantly reimagining our industries, building new products, and refining how we operate. We do our best work together. That’s why all of our office-based teams work onsite, five days a week. The base salary range for this role is $190,000 per year. Actual compensation will be determined on an individual basis and may vary depending on experience, skills, and qualifications. Base salary is just one part of your total rewards package. You may also be eligible for equity awards and an annual performance-based bonus. Benefits Summary (USA Full-Time Exempt Employees): Medical, dental, and vision insurance (multiple plans, incl. HSA options). Company-paid life and disability insurance (short- and long-term). Voluntary insurance: accident, critical illness, hospital indemnity. Optional supplemental life insurance for self, spouse, and children. Pet insurance discount. 401(k). Health Savings Account (HSA) Flexible Spending Accounts (Healthcare, Dependent Care, Commuter) Time Off policies: Discretionary vacation days 8 paid holidays per year Paid sick time Paid Bereavement leave Paid Parental Leave Benefits are subject to change at the company's discretion. Atoms accepts applications on an ongoing basis. &nbsp; Ready to join us as we serve those who serve others?&nbsp; #LI-Onsite

Who We Are: At Avnet, relationships matter. We are a global, FORTUNE ® 500 technology distributor and solutions company that delivers design, supply chain and logistics expertise to customers at every stage of a product’s lifecycle. Our employees have a front row seat to the latest innovations shaping the world we live in and the future we share. Job Summary Develops and maintains data pipelines and scalable data-processing solutions that transform diverse source data into trusted, usable information for business applications, analytics, and AI. Integrates, cleanses, models, and enriches large datasets from enterprise systems, APIs, external providers, and unstructured documents. Applies data engineering and machine learning techniques to improve data quality, automate classification and extraction, and support reusable, governed data products that advance business initiatives. Uses Python, SQL, and distributed processing frameworks such as Apache Spark to prepare and process large datasets in cloud platforms such as Databricks. Supports the development, testing, and application of machine learning models for product classification, attribute mapping, data enrichment, and other automation use cases. Processes structured and unstructured content, including product records, technical documents, and associated digital assets, using programmatic extraction and AI-assisted methods. Builds and maintains data models and curated datasets that support downstream applications, search, analytics, and AI solutions. Monitors pipeline execution and data quality; investigates failures, resolves discrepancies, and contributes to improvements in processing performance and reliability. Applies established data governance, access-control, metadata, and documentation standards to support traceable and maintainable data solutions. Collaborates with technical teams and business stakeholders to translate requirements into practical data solutions and communicate results, limitations, and business value. Other duties as assigned. Job Level Specifications Foundational knowledge of data engineering, programming, database concepts, and machine learning , acquired through academic instruction, projects, or relevant work experience. Develops solutions for defined data-processing and integration tasks using established architectures, tools, and engineering practices. Performs assigned work with increasing independence, exercising judgment in troubleshooting and implementation. Receives guidance on complex technical decisions, with work reviewed for accuracy and quality. Collaborates with team members and stakeholders to understand data requirements, resolve issues, and deliver usable results. Contributes to the quality and reliability of assigned pipelines, datasets, and analytical solutions; recognizes when issues require escalation or additional expertise. Work Experience: Typically less than 2 years with bachelor's or equivalent. Education and Certification(s): Bachelor's degree or equivalent experience from which comparable knowledge and job skills can be obtained. Distinguishing Characteristics: Hands-on experience with Python and SQL , including academic, internship, or project-based experience. Experience with data transformation, relational databases, and automated data processing. Exposure to Databricks, Apache Spark, cloud data platforms, machine learning libraries, APIs, and version control preferred. What We Offer: Our employees work hard to live our values and help us grow. Our total rewards strategy supports Avnet’s ability to attract, engage, develop, and reward our employees, while promoting a diverse and inclusive environment. We offer competitive compensation and benefit programs — from time away and flexible working arrangements to programs supporting employee well-being and opportunities to give back to your community.
