### Job description

As an organization, Google maintains a portfolio of research projects driven by fundamental research, new product innovation, product contribution and infrastructure goals, while providing individuals and teams the freedom to emphasize specific types of work. As a Research Scientist, you'll setup large-scale tests and deploy promising ideas quickly and broadly, managing deadlines and deliverables while applying the latest theories to develop new and improved products, processes, or technologies. From creating experiments and prototyping implementations to designing new architectures, our research scientists work on real-world problems that span the breadth of computer science, such as machine (and deep) learning, data mining, natural language processing, hardware and software performance analysis, improving compilers for mobile platforms, as well as core search and much more. As a Research Scientist, you'll also actively contribute to the wider research community by sharing and publishing your findings, with ideas inspired by internal projects as well as from collaborations with research programs at partner universities and technical institutes all over the world. Google's Quantum Error Correction team brings quantum error correction from theory to reality. We drive experimental QEC advances and enable research with QEC benchmarks and data, and collaborate across Quantum AI to conduct system integration tests and pave the way to large-scale error-corrected quantum computers. As a Research Scientist, you will contribute to our next generation of dynamic QEC experiments by driving system design, methodology, and real-time feedback testing. You will partner across theory, software, and device architecture, applying experimental quantum expertise to this exciting new domain. Google Quantum AI is building quantum computing for otherwise unsolvable problems. The full potential of quantum computing will be unlocked with a large-scale computer capable of complex, error-corrected computations. Our roadmap is focused on advancing the capabilities of quantum computing and enabling meaningful applications. Individual pay is determined by factors including job-related skills, experience, and relevant education or training. US: $174000 - $252000 (USD) + 15% bonus target + equity + benefits Learn more about benefits at Google . Design and implement system for running Quantum Error Correction experiments with real-time feedback. Collaborate with partner teams across a wide range of subfields to test new techniques, adopt them into standard procedures, and conduct system integration tests. Collaborate on interdisciplinary quantum error correction projects. Complete research projects and present results internally and externally. Minimum qualifications: PhD in physics, electrical engineering, quantum information, or equivalent experience. Experience calibrating, operating, or benchmarking quantum systems. Experience in research, including research projects, publishing papers, or presenting at conferences. Preferred qualifications: Experience with quantum error correction. Experience working on a team developing excellent tools that can be transferred to a production environment. Experience with cryogenic hardware, microwave engineering, electronics, or superconducting qubits. Strong Python proficiency for scientific computing and data analysis, including collaborative development and version control. PhD in physics, electrical engineering, quantum information, or equivalent experience. Experience calibrating, operating, or benchmarking quantum systems. Experience in research, including research projects, publishing papers, or presenting at conferences.

Master Data Analyst Your Manager: Master Data Lead Team: Planning, Supply Chain Location: Kilmalid, Dumbarton – we operate a hybrid working approach Contract Type: Fixed Term Contract – ending September 2027 Pernod Ricard is a worldwide leader in the spirits and champagne industry, blending traditional craftsmanship, state-of-the-art brand development, and global distribution technologies. Our prestigious portfolio of premium to luxury brands includes Absolut vodka, Ricard pastis, Ballantine’s, Chivas Regal, Royal Salute, and The Glenlivet Scotch whiskies, Jameson Irish whiskey, Martell cognac, Havana Club rum, Beefeater gin, Malibu liqueur, and Mumm and Perrier-Jouët champagnes. Our mission is to ensure the long-term growth of our brands with full respect for people and the environment, while empowering our employees around the world to be ambassadors of our purposeful, inclusive, and responsible culture of authentic conviviality Chivas Brothers is the Pernod Ricard business dedicated entirely to Scotch whisky. Our award-winning portfolio features some of the world’s most revered single malt and blended Scotch whisky brands, including Chivas, Ballantine’s, Royal Salute, The Glenlivet and Aberlour, which are exported from Scotland to over 100 countries worldwide. As the world’s No.2 Scotch whisky producer with a 1,600-strong team across 26 sites, we are committed to upholding the heritage and pursuing the progress of Scotch and ensuring its sustainable future, as well as opening it up to new audiences across the globe. About the role We are now seeking a Master Data Analyst to play a key part in maintaining accurate and timely product and Bill of Materials (BoM) data within the (Enterprise Resource Planning) ERP system. This role provides effective administrative support to the Master Data BOM team, including cased goods, spirit, material and BoM data input, set‑up and approval. The position ensures ERP system manufacturing setup of SKU’s and components is correct and accurate to support successful delivery of all R&D and change projects. The role works across the supply chain to deliver excellent customer service and improve cost, quality and compliance performance, while effectively communicating with departments such as Engineering, Distillation & Maturation, Inventory, Procurement, Research & Development, Customer Service, Planning, Outsourcing Bottling/Rework, Spirit Planning and MIS. What you will do SKU & Component Setup, Maintenance & Lifecycle Management Setup and maintain SKUs, Spirits and Components in line with standard practice. Action SKU Register requests to ensure Market Supply SKU Requests are delivered on time. Transact and expedite system workflow and support the Product Life Cycle Forum to ensure items are developed on time for bottling. Develop clear procedures, checks and measures to support accurate data setup and continuous improvement. Manage the Run-in/Run-out and PLC process to minimise obsolescence during the SKU lifecycle and during introduction of new and exit of old components and SKUs. Analyse all BOM change requests to ensure that each level of the multi-level BOM is changed in accordance with the requested change and ensure that the change request is necessary. Obsolete items as appropriate and mass-maintain on system to eliminate future exposure. Bill of Materials (BoM) Creation, Changes & Configuration Effectively configure and process Bill of Material as appropriate Set-up data for multi-level BOM’s Process and distribute information (Bill of Material / Design Changes) in a timely manner (OTIF) Attend and add valid input to project meeting to ensure new Bill of Materials build and cost are correct. Data Integrity & Accuracy Action data integrity reports daily and develop new integrity reports to ensure data accuracy. Work with colleagues to maintain accurate and reliable item and BoM data across the system. Work with relevant teams to investigate data issues affecting Bottling or Shipping and agree appropriate improvements. Cross Functional Support & Collaboration Support and contribute to Market Supply, Research & Development, Rework, Procurement and Manufacturing initiatives to improve service. Deliver excellent customer service, Work collaboratively to resolve issues, seeking support or escalating where appropriate. Stand in for line manager and represent the team and company professionally at project meetings. Reporting, Workflow & Continuous Improvement Collaborate with colleagues to identify and deliver opportunities for continuous improvement across the Master Data BOM team and wider Supply Chain. Manage the workflow process to ensure timely input of key data for new SKU builds and smooth progression through to live. Your skills and background Experience using business enterprise systems, such as JDE E1 ERP, and an understanding of BoM structures. Good planning and material process skills, with an organised and methodical approach. Comfortable using Microsoft Excel, Word and Outlook, with opportunities to further develop your skills. Experience in managing data accuracy and validation processes. Understanding of commercial, quality, and financial impacts of SKU setup. Why join us? Collective Spirit You will be part of a connected, international community shaped by iconic brands, a performance mindset, and a proud legacy, where collaboration, fun and ambition coexist. Craft your path We empower you to own your career, innovate boldly and grow alongside our business, surrounded by leaders who value curiosity, energy, and impact. Cultivate the future You’ll help shape a business with a soul — one that cherishes the land, respects communities, and brings good times from a good place, responsibly and sustainably. People are at the heart of everything we do at Pernod Ricard and are the foundation of our collective spirit as ‘Créateurs de Convivialité’. We promote inclusion throughout our business and supply chain. In our teams, we proactively foster a diverse work force and an inclusive culture, an environment where we can truly be ourselves. We will assess your application for this role based on your skills, experiences, and achievements, not your gender, age, ethnic or educational background or any other such factors. Our recruiting team are more than happy to provide reasonable adjustments during our application and interview process, in order for you to participate fully in our recruitment experience. Our mix of benefits Competitive salary, along with performance related bonus Defined Contribution Pension Private medical insurance, including income protection Comprehensive Employee Assistance Programme 25 days’ annual holiday plus public holidays Pernod Ricard share incentive plan and share ownership plan Life Assurance An allowance to spend on our brands Our reward & recognition scheme Employee discounts from a range of high street retailers Regular events in our very own office bar Learn more about working with Pernod Ricard [...]

### Job description

Job Title Research Associate Agency Texas A&M Agrilife Research Department Dallas Proposed Minimum Salary Commensurate Job Location Dallas, Texas Job Type Staff Job Description About Texas A&M AgriLife Texas A&M AgriLife is comprised of the following Texas A&M University System members: Texas A&M AgriLife Extension Service Texas A&M AgriLife Research College of Agriculture and Life Sciences at Texas A&M University Texas A&M Forest Service Texas A&M Veterinary Medical Diagnostic Laboratory As the nation’s largest most comprehensive agriculture program, Texas A&M AgriLife brings together a college and four state agencies focused on agriculture and life sciences within The Texas A&M University System. With over 5,000 employees and a presence in every county across the state, Texas A&M AgriLife is uniquely positioned to improve lives, environments and the Texas economy through education, research, extension and service. Click here to learn more about joining AgriLife! Job Summary: The Turfgrass Breeding program https://dallas.tamu.edu/research/turf/ at Texas A&M AgriLife Research and Extension Center in Dallas https://dallas.tamu.edu/ invites applications for a Research Associate to join an interdisciplinary research program. The position will contribute to the development and phenotypic characterization of warm- and cool-season turfgrasses with enhanced adaptation, biotic and abiotic stress tolerance, and turfgrass quality. This position provides an opportunity to work across the continuum from crossing and population development to phenotyping leading to new and improved cultivar development. This position is funded through external grants and/or contracts, which are subject to renewal at the discretion of the grantor or contracting agency. Continued employment is contingent upon the ongoing availability and renewal of funding, as well as employees' satisfactory job performance. Responsibilities: Hybridization (inter- and intra-) to develop new hybrids in zoysiagrass and St. Augustinegrass Data collection (field and greenhouse) for various morphological, seasonal, biotic and abiotic traits Assist and coordinate in the design and conduct of the experiment, statistically analyze data, and write draft manuscripts for publications Implement research protocols, which include plot setup, treatment application and data collection Procurement of supplies from vendors Supervision of part-time/wage-payroll employees assisting with greenhouse maintenance of turfgrass breeding material Management of trials/breeding nurseries, as needed Other duties assigned Qualifications: Required Education and Experience: M.S. degree in Plant Breeding, Genetics, Agronomy, Turfgrass Science or related field. Familiarity with appropriate laboratory, farm and/or technical equipment. Ability to effectively utilize a computer and applicable software to create databases, perform statistical analyses, present data and perform other computer related tasks. Ability to communicate effectively. Ability to establish effective working relationships. Ability to multi-task and work cooperatively with others. Preferred Qualifications: The ideal candidate will have a M.S degree with 2-years’ experience in the field or a Ph.D. in Plant Breeding, Genetics, Agronomy, Turfgrass Science, or a related field. Strong statistical skills are highly desirable. Experience with crossing techniques is preferred. Other Requirements: Travels to various field site locations. Ability to lift or move light, moderate, or heavy objects. Ability to work outdoors in variable weather conditions. What You Need to Know Salary: Compensation is commensurate with the candidate’s qualifications. Position Funding: This is funded through grants or contracts. Funding for this position is anticipated through December 31, 2029. Employment is contingent upon the continued availability of funding and satisfactory job performance. Flexible Work Arrangements: This position is not eligible for remote work. Important Notice Regarding H1-B Sponsorship: Your employment at Texas A&M AgriLife may require nonimmigrant sponsorship. A Presidential proclamation issued on September 19, 2025, imposes a $100,000 fee on new H-1B petitions filed after September 21, 2025. Texas A&M AgriLife will NOT pay this fee. If the fee is imposed on your petition, this offer of employment will be revoked. If actions taken during your employment result in the imposition of this fee, your employment will be terminated. To the extent that this statement conflicts with any applicable System or member Policy, Regulation, or Rule, this statement will control. We recommend that you consult with your private immigration counsel at your own expense regarding potential implications. In addition, on January 27, 2026, Texas Governor Abbott issued a moratorium on the filing of any new H-1B unless approved by the Texas Workforce Commission. Accordingly, if you will now or in the future require sponsorship for employment visa status this moratorium may affect our ability to employ you should you be selected as the final candidate. Benefits and Leave: Health, dental, vision, life and long-term disability insurance (AgriLife contributes to premiums) 12–15 annual paid holidays Up to 8 hours/month sick leave and at least 8 hours/month vacation Automatic enrollment in the Teacher Retirement System of Texas Employee Wellness Initiative Access to ongoing professional development and training through Aspire, LinkedIn Learning, and internally developed programs How to Apply Applications must include all job application data or a complete resume. Incomplete applications may be rejected. Applicants are encouraged to upload a resume or use LinkedIn to prepopulate application fields. Required Documents: CV / Resume Cover Letter List of References Certifications / Additional Documentation (if applicable) All positions are security-sensitive. Applicants are subject to a criminal history investigation, and employment is contingent upon the institution’s verification of credentials and/or other information required by the institution’s procedures, including the completion of the criminal history check. Equal Opportunity/Veterans/Disability Employer.

Ability to act independently and communicate effectively within a team. Ability to maintain a flexible schedule, including working on occasional evenings and weekends when needed. Exemplary communication skills (both written and oral) evidenced by experience preparing comprehensive research reports and grant applications incorporating complex, highly technical information. Highly developed organizational and leadership skills. Experience collaborating with other researchers and supervising research staff. The above statements are intended to describe the general nature and level of work being performed. They are not intended to be construed as an exhaustive list of all responsibilities, duties and skills required of personnel so classified. Staff Term (Fixed Term) Salary range $89,100.00 - $120,400.00 Salary commensurate with education and experience. ABOUT RETELL AI

Retell AI is using first-principles thinking to reimagine the call center with cutting-edge voice AI. Thousands of companies now use Retell's AI voice agents to handle sales, support, and logistics calls that once required large teams of human agents. Backed by Y Combinator, Alt Capital, and other leading investors. We've grown 20x from $5M to $100M+ in annualized revenue run rate in under two years, reaching a $1.5B+ valuation.

Our vision for 2026 is to build a modern CX platform where entire contact centers are powered by AI. Instead of basic automation that needs constant human tuning, we're creating intelligent AI “workers” that act as frontline agents, QA analysts, and managers, continuously executing, monitoring, and improving every customer interaction.

We're growing fast and looking for ambitious builders who want to tackle hard technical problems, move quickly, and have a real impact on one of the fastest-growing voice AI companies in the world.

Let's build the future together.

 - Voice AI Startup Hits $100 Million Run Rate With $4.6 Million Funding https://www.forbes.com/sites/timkeary/2026/11/12/voice-ai-startup-hits-100-million--run-rate-with-46-million-funding/

 - #3 Fastest-Growing Software Company, G2 Best Software Awards 2026 https://company.g2.com/news/g2s-2026-best-software-awards

 - #4 Fastest-Growing Software Vendor, Brex Benchmark 2025 https://www.brex.com/journal/brex-benchmark-december-2025

 - Enterprise Tech 30 Class of 2026, Nasdaq & Wing VC https://www.nasdaq.com/videos/retell-ai

 - Top-Ranked Startup, Lean AI Leaderboard https://leanaileaderboard.com/

 - Backed by Y Combinator https://www.ycombinator.com/companies/retell-ai

 


ABOUT THE ROLE

Retell AI is transforming customer experience with Voice AI for enterprises, including customers like CVS/Aetna, American Airlines, Lenovo, and Grab. We're building the infrastructure that powers millions of AI-powered phone conversations every month—and we're just getting started.

We're hiring an Applied AI Engineer to work directly with Spanish speaking enterprise customers to design, build, and deploy production AI voice applications.

As an Applied AI Engineer, you'll sit at the intersection of AI, software engineering, and customer success. You'll partner closely with customer engineering teams to integrate Retell into their products, solve complex technical challenges, optimize AI workflows, and help customers successfully launch and scale Voice AI in production.

This is an ideal role for someone who enjoys building software, solving ambiguous technical problems, and working directly with customers. You'll collaborate daily with Engineering, Product, and Customer Success while helping shape the future of enterprise AI.

This is a full-time position based in our Redwood City headquarters.

BUILD AI SOLUTIONS WITH CUSTOMERS

 - Design, build, and deploy production AI voice applications.

 - Partner with customer engineering teams to integrate Retell APIs and SDKs.

 - Identify recurring customer pain points and propose scalable solutions.

 - Help define best practices for deploying enterprise Voice AI.

 - Strong programming skills in Python, TypeScript, or similar languages.

 - Experience working with APIs, SDKs, and distributed systems.

 - Excellent debugging and problem-solving skills.

 - Strong communication skills and the ability to work directly with technical customers.

 - Available to work standard U.S. business hours with overlap with our Pacific Time team and enterprise customers.
   
    


NICE TO HAVE

 - Experience building AI or LLM-powered applications.

 - Experience with Voice AI, speech technologies, or conversational AI.

 - Experience with cloud infrastructure (AWS, GCP, Azure).

 - Experience with telephony, SIP, Twilio, or real-time communications.

 - Startup experience.

 - Customer-facing engineering, solutions engineering, or consulting experience.

 - Willingness to travel up to 10% for customer workshops, launches, and onsite engagements.
   
    


YOU MIGHT THRIVE IF

 - You enjoy building software that solves real customer problems.

 - You like working directly with engineers and technical stakeholders.

 - You enjoy owning projects from architecture through deployment.

 - You're excited about AI and love experimenting with the latest models and technologies.

 - You thrive in fast-moving startup environments with lots of ownership.

 - You enjoy wearing multiple hats across engineering, product, and customer success.

 - You care deeply about delivering exceptional customer outcomes while building scalable solutions.
   
    


JOB DETAILS

### Job description

Shirley Ryan AbilityLab is the global leader in physical medicine and rehabilitation for adults and children with the most severe, complex conditions. By joining our team, you’ll be part of our life-changing mission and vision. You’ll contribute to an innovative, multifaceted culture that is second to none — one that embraces collaboration, excellence, discovery and compassion. You’ll play a role in something that’s never been done before as we integrate science and clinical care to help patients achieve better, faster outcomes — as we Advance Human Ability, together. Job Description Summary The Research Assistant will be responsible for conducting literature searches, data management, recruiting participants, obtaining consents, maintaining files, scheduling and conducting interviews of patients and family members, maintaining data collection files, assisting with data analysis and generating correspondence, reports and graphics. The Research Assistant will consistently demonstrate support of the Shirley Ryan AbilityLab statement of Vision, Mission and Core Values by striving for excellence, contributing to the team efforts and showing respect and compassion for patients and their families, fellow employees, and all others with whom there is contact at or in the interest of the institute. The Research Assistant will demonstrate Shirley Ryan AbilityLab Core Attributes: Communication, Accountability, Flexibility/Adaptability, Judgment/Problem Solving, Customer Service and Core Values (Hope, Compassion, Discovery, Collaboration, & Commitment to Excellence) while fulfilling job duties. Job Description Responsibilities will vary according to the project in which the RA is assigned, but could include any or all of the following: Subject Recruitment Contacts hospital-site staff to identify potential participants; reviews medical records to determine appropriateness of patients for study. Explains study to patients and family members, requests participation and obtains informed consent Schedules and confirms participants for hospital and/or home interviews Data Collection Conducts on and off site interviews using a variety of structured and non-structured questionnaires Retrieves and records data from medical records and records data from interviews on appropriate forms Uses clinical judgment to alert supervisor in a timely manner that participant responses may indicate need for professional intervention Maintains interim contact with study participants to maintain current address and telephone information Dissemination Assists in development of data tables, graphs and charts and preparation of material for presentation Data Management Assists in the collection of new data; codes, cleans, transcribes, records, enters and stores all data collected; coordinates data collection with other research staff as appropriate Generates reports detailing data collected Conducts literature searches, copying and distributing articles as appropriate Transcribes and codes taped interviews Maintains an inventory of data collection forms to ensure ease of data retrieval Assists in analysis of qualitative and quantitative data by coding verbatim interviews Performs various record-keeping tasks such as filing consent forms and other data collection forms on all eligible participants to ensure ease of data retrieval and tracking of participants Supports Scholarly Activities Attends meetings; takes minutes as assigned Perform all other duties that may be assigned in the best interest of the Shirley Ryan AbilityLab. Reporting Relationships Reports directly to Lab Manager, Research Scientist, or Project Director Knowledge, Skills & Abilities Required Work requires communication and mathematics skills normally acquired in four years of college, plus three to six months of related work experience. Prefer graduate of or student in graduate program in neurosciences, nursing, psychology, engineering, social work, public health or related field. Knowledge of basic computer hardware in addition to software such as Microsoft Office, statistical programs such as Stata or SPSS, and/or relevant engineering or mathematical programs such as Matlab, as appropriate. Familiarity with medical, engineering or health services research terminology and basic research/statistical methodology is required, as appropriate to the position. Ability to accurately review and enter project data and to concentrate and pay close attention to detail for up to 75% of work time is required. Analytical ability is required to prepare reports, maintain data inventory, code, transcribe interviews, develop graphs, perform literature searches, negotiate systems in several hospitals and/or human service organizations, and identify concerns/responses of participants that require termination of interview or possible intervention. Strong interpersonal skills are needed to interact effectively with patients/family member dyads, physicians, other human service professionals and a variety of supervisors for various research projects. Flexibility to coordinate scheduling with other project staff is needed. Proficient typing and administrative skills to transcribe data, prepare correspondence, and maintain files. Exercises independent judgment in assigned area of responsibility. May be expected to take leadership on certain project tasks. Working Conditions Normal office/research laboratory environment with little or no exposure to dust or extreme temperature. May involve travel to other hospital/research sites and possibly to participants’ homes. Interviews/experiments may require flexible day, evening and/or weekend hours. The above statements are intended to describe the general nature and level of work being performed by people assigned to this classification. They are not intended to be construed as an exhaustive list of all responsibilities, duties and skills required of personnel so classified. ### Job description

Department PME ChemMatCARS About the Department NSF’s ChemMatCARS, affiliated with the Pritzker School of Molecular Engineering (PME), the University of Chicago and funded by NSF, is a national user facility for frontier research in chemistry and materials science using synchrotron x-rays. It is located at the Advanced Photon Source, Argonne National Laboratory. The mission of ChemMatCARS is to develop and operate world-class synchrotron x-ray facilities in the areas of small-molecule crystallography, liquid interface scattering, and anomalous small and wide-angle scattering to serve the needs of ChemMatCARS users. Job Summary The Pritzker School of Molecular Engineering is seeking a Research Professional to support the day-to-day operational and administrative needs of a large and complex research user facility at ChemMatCARS. The successful candidate will be responsible for supporting scientific users conducting research and providing mail-in and remote data collection services at ChemMatCARS. The candidate is also expected to initiate and engage in collaborative research with ChemMatCARS users and staff, while pursuing independent research activities. Additional responsibilities include the development, commissioning, and maintenance of advanced synchrotron X-ray instrumentation and experimental capabilities. Responsibilities Facilitates and promotes advanced technical/scientific research projects including data analysis. Recognizes opportunities for innovation and develops or incorporates new research concepts, methodologies, and instrumentation. Provides day-to-day operational support for ChemMatCARS user programs, including onsite, remote, and mail-in experiments. Assists in the preparation of grant proposals, progress reports, scientific presentations, and manuscripts. Works closely with senior researchers to develop documentation, experimental protocols, and computational resources. Maintains scientific operations, including coordination of students, postdoctoral researchers, research staff, and visiting users. Collaborates with ChemMatCARS staff and external users on multidisciplinary research projects. Creates and presents tutorials, workshops, and documentation for user training. Maintains laboratory records, inventories, websites, grant-related documentation, and operational reports. Reviews laboratory protocols and training on new techniques. Manage complex data sets for research. Trains new laboratory personnel. Performs other related work as needed. Minimum Qualifications Education: Minimum requirements include a PhD in related field. Work Experience: Minimum requirements include knowledge and skills developed through 2-5 years of work experience in a related job discipline. Certifications: --- Preferred Qualifications Education: Doctoral degree in materials science, chemistry, physics, chemical engineering, crystallography, or a related field. Experience: Significant work experience; postdoc experience preferred. Familiar with synchrotron X-ray scattering, diffraction, crystallography, or related experimental techniques. Supporting scientific users at a national laboratory or shared research facility is highly desirable. Demonstrated ability to initiate and lead collaborative research projects with students, postdoctoral researchers, and scientific staff. Background working in academic, national laboratory, or multidisciplinary research environments. Supervisory or mentoring experience is desirable. Technical Skills or Knowledge: Strong programming skills in Python; experience with C/C++ is desirable. Familiarity with scientific instrumentation, automation, and data acquisition systems. Knowledge of Linux-based operating systems. Familiarity with scientific data analysis and visualization software. Strong computer skills, including document management, spreadsheets, and databases. Familiarity with grant writing, manuscript preparation, and scientific communication. Knowledge of modern synchrotron X-ray instrumentation, crystallographic methods, or related experimental techniques. Knowledge of laboratory safety procedures and applicable federal regulations. Preferred Competencies Demonstrated administrative, problem-solving, organization and coordination skills. Demonstrated excellent oral and written communication skills. Excellent time management skills and handle multiple, concurrent tasks within deadlines with minimal supervision. Prioritize urgent tasks while ensuring established deadlines are met. Flexible and adaptable to changes in workflow and procedures. Handle stressful, sensitive, and confidential situations and information with absolute discretion. Knowledge and experience of related research techniques or methods. Knowledge of and familiarity with local and federal regulations, guidelines, and procedures. Application Documents Resume/CV, including a list of publications (required) Research Statement (required). References Contact Information (3)(required) The University of Chicago uses AI-assisted tools to streamline and augment some recruitment processes; however, AI is not used to make hiring decisions. When applying, the document(s) MUST be uploaded via the My Experience page, in the section titled Application Documents of the application. - Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

This is an early-career engineering job where your work ends up in front of the leadership of a global company. You'll build AI tools for Pfizer's Office of the Chief Executive Officer (OCEO), which sets the company's strategy, looks after its communications and global health investments, and runs the CEO's own office. You'll take ideas from a first conversation to something senior colleagues rely on. You'll work directly with the people who had the ideas. Many of them aren't engineers, and you'll often be the one sitting next to them while the idea turns into something real. You'll be building with current AI models and tools from your first week, with a senior engineering lead guiding you, and you'll own solutions yourself as you get more experienced. There are two of these jobs, in a team of five, and you'd report to the Senior Director, AI Innovation and Delivery. It suits someone who likes learning in the open, wants to see what their work actually does, and is as happy helping a colleague build something as building it alone. Role Responsibilities Pair with the people who have ideas Work alongside colleagues who bring an idea in, especially if it's their first time. Help them shape it and test it, be straight about what it will take to make it production-grade, and make sure they know what they own once it's live. Next time, they should need you a bit less. Build and harden Build features, integrations and fixes from the briefs the Product Lead agrees with originators. You'll use AI coding tools, and your work gets reviewed. Some ideas you'll help build from scratch; others start as a prototype that you turn into a production-grade solution. Before writing anything new, check whether it already exists. Test and evaluate Write the tests and evaluation cases that show whether something works. Run them before release and keep them running after. Watch how solutions behave in real use, find where they get things wrong, and turn what you find into fixes or new tests. Help run what we build Take your share of the day-to-day running: keeping an eye on quality and cost, dealing with issues, applying platform updates, keeping releases tidy. If something looks off, flag it early instead of quietly working around it. Learn it, then own it Get to know the solutions the team runs, including work handed over by outside partners, well enough to change them with confidence. As you get more experienced, you'll take ownership of particular ones. Document as you go Keep how-to guides, decision notes and runbooks up to date, and write them so people outside the team can follow them too. It's what keeps a solution working after the people who built it have moved on. Role Behaviours Help people build Leave each person you work with more capable than before. A pairing session went well if they can do more on their own afterwards. Ask early Ask questions and raise problems as soon as they come up, whether it's the code, the data or getting access to it. A question on day one costs far less than a mistake found at release. Reuse first Look for a component, pattern or platform feature that already does the job. Writing something new needs a reason. Test what you ship A change isn't finished until you've shown it works on real examples. Keep that evidence where others can see it. Write it down Note what you built and why, in plain words a colleague outside the team can follow. If you're the only one who can explain it, it's not done yet. Work Location Assignment: Hybrid Purpose Breakthroughs that change patients' lives ... At Pfizer we are a patient centric company, guided by our four values: courage, joy, equity and excellence. Our breakthrough culture lends itself to our dedication to transforming millions of lives. Digital Transformation Strategy One bold way we are achieving our purpose is through our company wide digital transformation strategy. We are leading the way in adopting new data, modelling and automated solutions to further digitize and accelerate drug discovery and development with the aim of enhancing health outcomes and the patient experience. Flexibility We aim to create a trusting, flexible workplace culture which encourages employees to achieve work life harmony, attracts talent and enables everyone to be their best working self. Let’s start the conversation! Equal Employment Opportunity We believe that a diverse and inclusive workforce is crucial to building a successful business. As an employer, Pfizer is committed to celebrating this, in all its forms – allowing for us to be as diverse as the patients and communities we serve. Together, we continue to build a culture that encourages, supports and empowers our employees. DisAbility Confident We are proud to be a Disability Confident Employer and we encourage you to put your best self forward with the knowledge and trust that we will make any reasonable adjustments necessary to support your application and future career. Our mission is unleashing the power of our people, especially those with unique superpowers. Your journey with Pfizer starts here! To learn more about acceptable and prohibited uses of AI during the recruitment process, please review our candidate AI-use guidelines available on Pfizer Careers . Information & Business Tech

### Job description

Who we are Atoms is building the machines that power the next era of progress. Over the last decade, software has transformed the digital world. But the physical world, where food is made, minerals are mined, goods are moved, and industries are run, remains far less intelligent, far less efficient, and far more constrained. We’re changing that. Atoms builds Physical AI real-world robots for the industries that move civilization forward, starting with food, mining, and transport. Our systems are designed to understand, predict, and control the real world with precision, turning complex physical operations into something more reliable, more scalable, and more productive. This work requires more than robotics. It requires deep integration across AI, hardware, software, operations, manufacturing, and real estate. We don’t just build machines in a lab. We deploy them into real environments, operate them, learn from them, and improve them until they work at scale. We are researchers, roboticists, engineers, and builders. We believe the next great technology companies will not only transform information, but the physical systems that shape everyday life. If you want to work on hard problems with real-world impact, join us. &nbsp; About the role We are seeking a full-time AI Researcher (New Grad) to join our founding team and bridge the gap between high-level AI research and real-world physical actuation for our next-generation autonomous transport platforms. In this role, you will contribute directly to core research initiatives on Perception, World Models, and Action models. You will help translate cutting-edge theoretical breakthroughs into scalable, real-world deployment on physical autonomous systems, driving how our platforms perceive, simulate, and navigate complex environments. &nbsp; What you’ll do Train, evaluate, and fine-tune multimodal models that map real-time sensor inputs and behavioral goals to vehicle actuation. Experiment with generative world model architectures using multi-sensor log data to improve simulation fidelity and trajectory querying. Prototype, benchmark, and scale reinforcement learning (RL), trajectory planning, and model distillation algorithms based on state-of-the-art literature. Integrate emerging AI research models into internal training and deployment pipelines, optimizing for latency and physical vehicle constraints. Systematically analyze edge cases and failure modes in novel, unstructured operating environments to iterate on model performance and stability. Design and run rigorous closed-loop simulation benchmarks alongside engineering and validation teams to identify bottlenecks and guide model improvements. Collaborate with cross-functional teams across hardware, systems, and robotics software to test and validate models on physical fleets. &nbsp; What we're looking for Education: Recent graduate or completing a Bachelor’s or Master’s degree in Computer Science, EECS, Robotics, AI, or a closely related quantitative field. Core Expertise: Coursework, substantial project experience, or research background focused on at least one of the following domains: Generative World Models / Video Generation / Diffusion Models Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models or embodied AI Reinforcement Learning (RL), trajectory optimization, or decision-making systems Large-scale multimodal Transformer architectures applied to robotics or spatio-temporal data Framework Fluency: Advanced hands-on proficiency with PyTorch or JAX for training, fine-tuning, and evaluating neural network architectures. Programming: Strong programming skills in Python and working knowledge of C++ (C++14 or newer). Preferred qualifications Prior internship, co-op, or published academic research experience in autonomous driving, mobile robotics, computer vision, or spatial AI. Experience working with simulation environments (e.g., Isaac Sim, CARLA) or robotic hardware platforms. Familiarity with model optimization techniques (e.g., quantization, TensorRT, ONNX) for edge compute deployment. &nbsp; Why join us At Atoms, you’ll work on one of the defining challenges of our time bringing automation into the physical world to drive real, lasting impact. We exist to uncover valuable unknown truths and turn them into progress, which means constantly pushing beyond what’s known and building what doesn’t yet exist. The work is ambitious and often challenging, but it’s grounded in a shared sense of purpose and a team committed to seeing it through together. Our work only matters if it serves others, and we know that meaningful progress depends on the trust of the people we serve and the strength of our team so we invest in both, creating an environment where you can do your best work and grow. &nbsp; What else you need to know This role is based in our Mountain View or San Francisco office. Atoms is a company driven by invention and continuous change, we are constantly reimagining our industries, building new products, and refining how we operate. We do our best work together. That’s why all of our office-based teams work onsite, five days a week. The base salary range for this role is $170,000 per year. Actual compensation will be determined on an individual basis and may vary depending on experience, skills, and qualifications. Base salary is just one part of your total rewards package. You may also be eligible for equity awards and an annual performance-based bonus. Benefits Summary (USA Full-Time Exempt Employees): Medical, dental, and vision insurance (multiple plans, incl. HSA options). Company-paid life and disability insurance (short- and long-term). Voluntary insurance: accident, critical illness, hospital indemnity. Optional supplemental life insurance for self, spouse, and children. Pet insurance discount. 401(k). Health Savings Account (HSA) Flexible Spending Accounts (Healthcare, Dependent Care, Commuter) Time Off policies: Discretionary vacation days 8 paid holidays per year Paid sick time Paid Bereavement leave Paid Parental Leave Benefits are subject to change at the company's discretion. Atoms accepts applications on an ongoing basis. &nbsp; Ready to join us as we serve those who serve others?&nbsp; #LI-Onsite

## 59. [MAYBE] CuspAI | Applied AI/ML Research Engineer (Generative Models)
- Link: https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/cuspai/ea10b5f7-1672-4294-a9d4-602403e67a89
- Location: Berlin, DE Germany remote
- Posted: Nov 17 10:35 ET

- Type: full-time

- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

ABOUT CUSPAI 

CuspAI is the frontier AI company on a mission to solve the breakthrough materials needed to power human progress. While nature took billions of years to perfect molecules, we are harnessing AI to unlock trillion-dollar materials breakthroughs in months, not millennia. Our founding team is the most cited in the world, comprised of world-class researchers in AI, chemistry and engineering.

We are working on some of the hardest and most important challenges including energy, clean water, the future of compute, and carbon capture, and this is just the start of what our 'search engine' for next-generation materials will unlock.

We invite you to be part of a diverse, innovative team at the intersection of AI and materials science, working to create impactful partnerships that drive innovation, scalability, and industry collaboration. This work matters. Your work matters.

We’re on the cusp of the on-demand materials era. Join us.




THE ROLE

We are looking for an Applied AI/ML Research Engineer* to build, train, and evaluate generative models in our materials discovery platform: diffusion, flow, and autoregressive models that generate material structures according to a prompt of target properties and design constraints.

 

*Note that you would be joining as a ‘Member of Technical Staff’, but the job title above hopefully helps to explain the nature of this role.

 

You will contribute to all aspects of model development: processing training data, defining evals, coming up with hypotheses, implementing models, building training infrastructure, and making sure that the final models are useful in real-world material discovery campaigns. You may be working with inorganic crystals, molecules, polymers, metal-organic compounds, and other material classes. You will be part of a small dedicated team, but interact with a large group of chemists, machine learning research, and engineers.

 


YOUR IMPACT

In this role you will be building novel generative models that transform how we design new materials, which is critical for accelerating the discovery of next-generation materials for energy and sustainability challenges.

 

The models you will build will be used to design materials for better solar cells, catalysts, semiconductors, batteries, carbon-capture sorbents, and so on.




WHAT YOU WILL DO


Generative Model Development & Evaluation

 - Develop, train, and rigorously evaluate generative models of material structures across different material classes and modalities.

 - Define and implement evaluations for material generators that measure what really matters for actual material discovery problems.

 - Assemble, process, and check material data that may be relevant for future models.

Integration, Engineering & Discovery Campaigns

 - Integrate your models into the wider CuspAI platform, ensuring they are robust, scalable, and accessible for material discovery workflows.

 - Collaborate with the software engineering team to adhere to best practices in coding, testing, and deployment.

 - Support material discovery campaigns that use your generative tools to identify promising candidates for real-world applications, for instance in solar cells, semiconductors, catalysts, or carbon capture.

 - Analyse the outputs of these campaigns to iteratively improve evals, data, and models.

Interdisciplinary Collaboration

 - Work together with the existing material generation team and the wider Cusp technical team, to align model capabilities with experimental realities.

 - Partner with material scientists and chemists to understand the physical constraints and properties required for valid and novel materials generation.




MUST HAVE SKILLS AND QUALIFICATIONS


 - Generative models: You possess deep experience building, training, and evaluating diffusion, flow, or autoregressive models, ideally at scale.

 - Software engineering: You are a strong software engineer and have experience developing code in a team. You take pride in building robust and maintainable tools ready for real-world use. You know your way around Python and either PyTorch or jax.

 - Collaborative spirit: You have a demonstrated ability to work well in a team, communicating complex technical concepts to colleagues from different scientific backgrounds.

 - Mission alignment: A genuine enthusiasm for using technology to do something good in the world and solve sustainability challenges.




BONUS POINTS (BUT NOT CRITICAL)


We would love you to have experience with at least one of the following:

 - Multimodal generation: You have built, trained, or evaluated multimodal generative models.

 - Reinforcement learning: You have experience using reinforcement learning, ideally in the post-training of generative models.

 - Domain expertise: You have experience with problems in chemistry or material science, for instance by previously building models in this domains. You know your perovskite from your polymer. A formal education or degree on these subjects is not required.




ADDITIONAL CONSIDERATIONS

This role could be based in our Amsterdam, London, Cambridge, or Berlin offices, with the expectation of being in the office three days per week. Additionally, there may be regular travel required to other locations for collaboration and project work.




WHAT WE OFFER

 - A competitive salary: We value and reward impact and growth

 - Equity in CuspAI: You have a stake in the success of the company

 - Time off to stay fresh: 28 days holiday (DE, NL, UK) or 21 days holiday (JP, SG, US), in addition to local public holidays

 - ‘Gold Standard’ parental leave: 26 weeks (primary caregiver) and 12 weeks (secondary caregiver) at full pay - we look after you and your family while we work on the most important materials discovery problems together

 - Professional development budget: We invest in your career development so you can stay up to date with the latest industry knowledge or add to your skills to increase impact and growth

 - Solve meaningful problems: See how your work has a direct impact on advancing materials science and solving sustainability and climate-related problems through the creation and application of bleeding-edge SOTA technology and revolutionary techniques

 - True interdisciplinary teamwork: Be part of a deeply collaborative environment bridging AI research, computational chemistry, and experimental science - work with world-class researchers and engineers who enjoy sharing knowledge and supporting each other


Join us in shaping the future of materials with AI. Together, we can create groundbreaking solutions for a more sustainable world.


CuspAI is an equal opportunities employer committed to building a diverse and inclusive workplace. We do not discriminate on the basis of sex, race, religion or belief, ethnic or national origin, disability, age, citizenship, marital, domestic or civil partnership status, sexual orientation, gender identity, pregnancy or related condition ( [...]

Position Description: The Temporary Research Assistant in the College of Education will support research, development, and professional development initiatives at the Kentucky Reading Research Center (KRRC), with an emphasis on emergent literacy and young children's reading and writing development. This position will assist with the development and testing of teacher observation systems, contribute to research publications, and support the identification and development of external funding opportunities. The Temporary Research Assistant will also collaborate with researchers, educators, and community partners to develop evidence-based professional development resources and instructional practices that promote early literacy development among preschool-aged children. The position requires strong research, analytical, organizational, and communication skills, as well as the ability to contribute to multiple research and programmatic activities. Essential Duties and Responsibilities 1. Research and Development of Teacher Observation Systems Assist with testing and establishing the reliability of a storybook observation system. Collaborate with research team members to develop and co-author one to two research publications related to teacher observation systems and emergent literacy. Support the development and refinement of an observation system to assess teacher practices that promote young children's writing development. 2. Grant Development and Funding Support Identify and research potential funding opportunities that align with the Center's research priorities and ongoing study designs. Contribute to the development of grant proposals, including supporting documentation, research descriptions, and other required materials. Assist with efforts to secure external funding to support continued research and program activities. 3. Professional Development and Instructional Support Support the development of professional development opportunities for preschool teachers focused on evidence-based instructional practices. Assist in developing resources and materials that promote young children's emergent reading and writing development. Collaborate with researchers and education partners to translate research findings into practical strategies that support early literacy instruction. Preferred Qualifications Master's degree in Education, Early Childhood Education, Literacy, Educational Psychology, Research Methods, or a related field. Experience supporting or conducting research related to emergent literacy, early childhood education, or early reading development. Familiarity with foundational literacy skills, including phonological awareness, alphabet knowledge, print concepts, oral language, and phonics. Experience administering, scoring, or interpreting early literacy assessments and screening instruments. Experience collecting, managing, analyzing, and reporting quantitative and/or qualitative research data. Familiarity with research methodologies, program evaluation, and evidence-based educational practices. Experience working with preschool and elementary-aged children, educators, school districts, or early childhood education programs. Familiarity with Institutional Review Board (IRB) requirements, research ethics, informed consent, and research protocols. Experience using statistical analysis software (e.g., SPSS, SAS, or R) or qualitative data analysis tools. Experience preparing research reports, literature reviews, presentations, and other scholarly or technical documents. Experience coordinating research activities, managing project timelines, and collaborating with multidisciplinary research teams. Competencies Strong written and verbal communication skills, including the ability to communicate effectively with researchers, educators, and community partners. Ability to work collaboratively with multidisciplinary research teams and manage multiple assignments and deadlines. Research and analytical skills. Knowledge of emergent literacy development. Data management and analysis. Attention to detail. Collaboration and relationship building. Organization and project coordination. Critical thinking and problem-solving. Compensation will be commensurate to candidate experience. Equal Employment Opportunity The University of Louisville

A Research Associate I-Bench position focusing on translational discoveries in T cell memory, exhaustion and cancer immunotherapy is available at the Sarkar-Kalia Lab. The project involves high dimensional immunomics approaches to make new discoveries, which feed seamlessly into our pipeline of next-generation engineered CAR T cell, bispecific T cell engager and checkpoint blockade immunotherapies for solid tumors in preclinical mouse models. Our studies aim to achieve metastasis-free and lifelong remission of intractable solid tumors such as pancreatic cancer, brain tumor, lung cancer, melanoma, neuroblastoma and sarcoma. Duties: Conduct laboratory work and experiments to support ongoing research projects in the realm of infectious disease and cancer immunology and immunotherapy. Work collaboratively with senior researchers and scientists to perform a variety of moderately complex technical procedures; implement modifications in experimental procedures or technique to obtain optimal experimental results; assist in the analysis, interpretation, organization and presentation of data; and maintain the laboratory inventory, equipment and resources. Together, we deliver superior patient care, advance new discoveries and treatments through pediatric research, and serve as the pediatric and adolescent, academic medical center for Washington, Alaska, Montana and Idaho – the largest region of any children’s hospital in the country. U.S. News & World Report consistently ranks Seattle Children’s among the nation’s best children’s hospitals. For more than a decade, Seattle Children’s has been nationally recognized in key specialty areas. We are honored to be one of the nation’s very best places to care for children and the top-ranked pediatric hospital in Washington and the Pacific Northwest. As a Magnet designated institution, we recognize the importance of hiring and developing great talent to provide best-in-class care to the patients and families we serve. Our organizational DNA takes form in our core values: Compassion, Excellence, Integrity, Collaboration, Equity and Innovation. Whether it’s delivering frontline care to our patients in a kind and caring manner, practicing the highest standards of quality and safety, or being relentlessly curious as we work towards eradicating childhood diseases, these values are the fabric of our culture and community. The future starts here. This is your chance to shape legendary play that lasts a lifetime. &nbsp; Senior Analyst, People Analytics (Temporary Assignment) We take play seriously. We’re looking for curious adventurers ready to find their party, fueled by imagination and drive to build what’s never been built before. At Hasbro and Wizards of the Coast, you’ll collaborate with passionate teams to reimagine our iconic brands and create experiences that spark joy, connection, and community through the magic of play. This is your chance to shape legendary play that lasts a lifetime. This is a temporary assignment through ManpowerGroup/Tapfin supporting Hasbro. Job Overview Hasbro is looking for a senior people analytics practitioner to own a live reporting and insights portfolio spanning employee engagement, talent acquisition, talent and succession, and the workforce measures reported to executive leadership. This is a hands-on analyst role. You will run the analysis yourself, present the findings to the audience that asked for them, and work with HR Systems and enterprise analytics to correct the data underneath. The portfolio carries recurring cycles with fixed dates alongside project work, and the requirement is reporting that is accurate and on time together with analysis that explains what the numbers mean and what should happen next. You will report to the Vice President, HR Planning, Analytics &amp; Technology, and work alongside a senior data analyst. You will partner with the HR business partners, the centers of excellence, Talent Acquisition, HR Systems and the enterprise analytics team, and your output reaches the HR leadership team, giving you a direct role in how people data shapes decisions. Candidates who have built their analytical experience through different paths are encouraged to apply. Location and Work Arrangement This role is based at our Boston, Massachusetts office and follows a hybrid schedule designed to support in-person collaboration. Employees are expected to work onsite on Tuesdays, Wednesdays, and Thursdays, with the flexibility to work remotely or onsite on Mondays and Fridays. What You'll Do Own the employee engagement survey cycle end to end, including administration, analysis, and the findings and action planning presented to HR leadership. Own talent acquisition analytics, including the recruiting dashboard, funnel and time to fill reporting, and offer acceptance methodology. Own talent and succession reporting, including the nine-box cycle, cohort movement across high-potential populations, and talent form completion reporting. Produce the quarterly workforce measures reported to the executive steering committee for the headquarters relocation, covering retention, offer acceptance, intern conversion and in-office attendance. Move recurring reporting toward self-service where the audience and the question are stable, reducing manual production effort. Agree the question, the audience and the intended decision with the requester before the analysis starts. Present analysis to senior HR stakeholders in clear business language, with the recommendation and the limitations stated. Challenge incoming requests on problem definition and on whether the available data can answer them. Reconcile and validate data across the human capital platform, the applicant tracking system and the reporting layer, investigate discrepancies to root cause, and work with the system owners to correct them at the source. Maintain metric definitions and the people data dictionary with HR Systems and enterprise analytics, so that a measure is calculated consistently across reports. Maintain documentation of report definitions, data sources, business logic and intended use. Apply confidentiality and access judgment when working with employee-level data, including where individual identification is appropriate. What You'll Bring Five or more years in people analytics, HR reporting or an equivalent analytical function, with a bachelor's degree in statistics, data science, analytics, economics, industrial and organizational psychology, human resources or a related field, or equivalent demonstrated experience. Hands-on depth in analysis, including statistical testing, cohort analysis and trend analysis, and the ability to select the method that fits the question. Expert Excel, including advanced formulas, pivot tables, lookups, data cleansing, conditional logic, data validation and reconciliation across large datasets. Working proficiency in SQL, enough to query, join, filter and aggregate across multiple tables and sources. Hands-on experience with an enterprise human capital platform and its reporting layer, SAP SuccessFactors and Power BI preferred, with equivalent platform depth considered. Experience administering and analyzing an employee listening or engagement survey platform. A record of presenting analysis to senior stakeholders and translating it into recommendations for a non-technical audience. Evidence of identifying data errors independently, investigating the cause and resolving them with the owning team rather than adjusting the output. Experience working to fixed reporting cycles alongside project work, and judgment in setting expectations when the two compete. What Sets You Apart Greenhouse or comparable applicant tracking system reporting. Python or R, and forecasting, scenario or predictive analysis. Experience building a metric dictionary or data governance standards for people data. Employment Details This is a temporary assignment supporting Hasbro. The selected individual will be employed and paid by ManpowerGroup/Tapfin as a W-2 employee and will not be an employee of Hasbro. Employment Type: Temporary Assignment Location: Boston, Massachusetts (Hybrid) Duration: 6-8 months Hourly Pay Range: $46.00 - $69.00 USD In compliance with local law, we are disclosing the hourly pay range for this role. Benefits through ManpowerGroup/Tapfin ManpowerGroup offers Consultants and their eligible spouses and children medical, dental, and vision coverage, as well as a health savings account, flexible spending accounts, a 401(k) plan and a Non-Qualified Savings Plan with a company match, supplemental life and disability insurance, business travel insurance, an employee assistance program, and wellbeing perks such as Active&amp;Fit Direct and BenefitHub discounts. Job Responsibilities Develop advanced agentic AI solutions across NLP, speech analytics, time series, reinforcement learning, and recommendation systems. Design robust agent architectures combining LLM reasoning with tools, structured data, and APIs—spanning state, memory, and context management, plus loop engineering (plan/act/observe, verification, termination, and fallback/escalation). Engineer reliable agent-driven workflows emphasizing correctness, traceability, and control-aware behavior (guardrails, approvals, auditable decision paths). Build knowledge-centric reasoning layers, including knowledge graphs and hybrid retrieval (RAG + graph + structured sources) to improve grounding and accuracy. Drive specification-driven development: author specs and contracts (schemas, validators, tool/skill interfaces) and build evaluation/regression harnesses. Advance agent quality via recursive self-improvement through automated evaluation and critique loops, red-team feedback, skill/prompt instruction optimization, and outcome-driven dataset curation (human-in-the-loop as needed). Design and implement multi-agent orchestration pipelines for SRE/observability use cases — integrating Dynatrace APM (problem APIs, golden signals, entity resolution), Splunk/SPL (Atlas CloudWatch and OnPrem query patterns), infrastructure metadata , code search, and JIRA ticket automation. Author agent specs as formal contracts: define phase-by-phase protocols, STRICT CONSTRAINTS, backflow/escalation signals, auth-error handling, and per-phase checkpoint schemas. Implement causal reasoning frameworks for evidence-based RCA and confidence scoring. Build A/B parity evaluation frameworks and regression harnesses to measure agent behavior changes across releases; define merge-gate criteria and scoreboard metrics. Develop MCP (Model Context Protocol) server integrations and plugin architectures for AI coding assistants (Claude Code). Implement per-application customization knowledge banks and agent memory systems that persist context across investigation phases. Coach and mentor AIML team members, setting a high bar for engineering rigor and research depth. Required Qualifications, Capabilities, and Skills Studies in a quantitative discipline ( CS/EE/Math/OR/Optimization/Data Science) or equivalent industry/research experience Demonstrated expertise building agentic AI systems, including several of: memory/state/context management, tool orchestration and workflow reliability patterns, loop engineering, spec-driven development, and prompt/skill instruction optimization. Strong hands-on experience with ML/DL methods and toolkits (e.g., PyTorch/TensorFlow plus core Python data/ML stack). Ability to design experiments and evaluation frameworks with metrics aligned to business outcomes (quality, reliability, latency, cost, safety). Experience with scalable data and model workflows (training and/or inference) and strong software engineering practices. Strong communication skills to explain technical concepts to both technical and business audiences. Hands-on experience with at least one observability/APM platform (Dynatrace, Splunk, Datadog, New Relic, or equivalent) — including writing queries, interpreting golden signals, and using APIs for automated incident triage. Proficiency in Python for pipeline scripting, automation, and agent tooling; comfort with JSON schema design and structured output validation. Familiarity with AWS services relevant to observability and agentic workloads (ECS, EKS, Lambda, CloudWatch, ALB, Step Functions) Familiarity with MCP (Model Context Protocol) server development or plugin authoring for AI coding assistants (Claude Code, GitHub Copilot, Cursor, or equivalent). Preferred Qualifications, Capabilities, and Skills Knowledge in search/ranking, reinforcement learning, or meta-learning (especially for agent routing, policies, and self-improvement). Experience with knowledge graphs, entity resolution, and ontology design. Experience with A/B experimentation and metric-driven product development; CI pipelines and unit/integration testing. Hands-on experience with Splunk SPL — writing complex search queries, index resolution, field extraction, and alert patterns for production observability. Experience with Dynatrace APIs — entity queries, problem feeds, golden signal retrieval, and synthetic monitor management. Experience with SRE practices: incident response workflows, root cause analysis methodology, SLOs/SLIs, on-call operations, or production readiness reviews Experience with GraphRAG, multi-hop retrieval, or knowledge-graph-augmented agent reasoning. Forward Deployed Engineering or Spec-Driven Development experience — translating operational workflows into formal agent specs with schemas, validators, and compliance audit trails. Experience building evaluation harnesses for long-horizon agentic tasks: golden-dataset construction, LLM-as-judge scoring, regression detection, and parity testing across agent versions.   JPMorganChase, one of the oldest financial institutions, offers innovative financial solutions to millions of consumers, small businesses and many of the world’s most prominent corporate, institutional and government clients under the J.P. Morgan and Chase brands. Our history spans over 200 years and today we are a leader in investment banking, consumer and small business banking, commercial banking, financial transaction processing and asset management. We offer a competitive total rewards package including base salary determined based on the role, experience, skill set and location. ### Job description

At U.S. Bank, we’re on a journey to do our best. Helping the customers and businesses we serve to make better and smarter financial decisions and enabling the communities we support to grow and succeed. We believe it takes all of us to bring our shared ambition to life, and each person is unique in their potential. A career with U.S. Bank gives you a wide, ever-growing range of opportunities to discover what makes you thrive at every stage of your career. Try new things, learn new skills and discover what you excel at—all from Day One. Job Description The successful candidate will leverage advanced quantitative methods, strong programming skills, and deep derivatives expertise to develop, enhance, and maintain counterparty exposure models used to support the Bank's trading, prime brokerage, and securities financing businesses. This role requires close collaboration with trading desks, risk management, technology partners, model validation, internal audit, and regulatory stakeholders. The ideal candidate will possess strong expertise in derivative pricing, stochastic simulation, and counterparty exposure modeling, with the ability to apply quantitative techniques to a broad range of products and portfolios. The modeling framework is primarily based on Monte Carlo simulation and derivative valuation methodologies and supports the measurement of key counterparty credit risk metrics such as Potential Future Exposure (PFE) and Expected Exposure (EE). Scope of Coverage The team's models support a broad range of products and businesses, including: Interest Rate Derivatives Foreign Exchange Derivatives Equity Derivatives Commodity Derivatives Prime Brokerage and Equity Finance Products Securities Financing Transactions These models are used to estimate and manage counterparty credit exposure in support of the Bank's Counterparty Credit Risk Management framework, regulatory requirements, and business growth initiatives. Key Responsibilities Research, design, develop, test, and maintain counterparty exposure models for derivatives, prime brokerage, and securities financing transactions. Develop and enhance methodologies for measuring Potential Future Exposure (PFE), Expected Exposure (EE), and other CCR risk metrics. Support the development and maintenance of pricing models and risk factor simulation frameworks used in Monte Carlo exposure calculations. Develop and enhance exposure methodologies for Prime Brokerage and Equity Finance products. Perform model monitoring, benchmarking, backtesting, sensitivity analysis, and performance assessments at the risk factor, trade, and portfolio levels. Partner closely with trading desks, risk managers, technology teams, and business stakeholders to ensure quantitative solutions effectively meet business, risk, and regulatory requirements. Collaborate with Model Risk Management, Internal Audit, and regulatory agencies to support model reviews, validation activities, and regulatory examinations. Prepare clear and comprehensive technical documentation covering model methodology, assumptions, limitations, implementation requirements, and performance monitoring results. Present quantitative methodologies, model performance results, and risk analytics to technical and non-technical audiences, including senior management and governance committees. Contribute to the ongoing enhancement of the Bank's counterparty credit risk modeling framework, analytics infrastructure, and quantitative capabilities. Basic Qualifications - Bachelor’s degree in a quantitative field, and 10 or more years of relevant experience OR - MA/MS in a quantitative field, and six or more years of related experience OR - PhD in a quantitative field, and five or more years of related experience Preferred Skills/Experience Master or Ph.D degree in a quantitative or related field (e.g., economics, finance, mathematics, statistics, engineering, computer science) or equivalent practical experience. Five or more years of experience in quantitative modeling, quantitative research, derivatives analytics, risk management, or a related field. Strong programming skills in Python, C++, or similar languages, including experience developing quantitative models and analytical libraries. Deep understanding of financial markets, derivative products, Prime Brokerage activities, and Securities Financing Transactions, including the key drivers of counterparty credit exposure. Demonstrated expertise in derivative pricing, stochastic processes, Monte Carlo simulation techniques, and quantitative risk modeling. Strong analytical and problem-solving abilities with exceptional attention to detail. Excellent written and verbal communication skills, including the ability to explain complex quantitative concepts to technical and non-technical stakeholders. LOCATION EXPECTATIONS: This role requires working from a U.S. Bank Location three (3) or more days per week. If there’s anything we can do to accommodate a disability during any portion of the application or hiring process, please refer to our disability accommodations for applicants . Benefits: Our approach to benefits and total rewards considers our team members’ whole selves and what may be needed to thrive in and outside work. That's why our benefits are designed to help you and your family boost your health, protect your financial security and give you peace of mind. Our benefits include the following: Healthcare (medical, dental, vision) Basic term and optional term life insurance Short-term and long-term disability Pregnancy disability and parental leave 401(k) and employer-funded retirement plan Paid vacation (from two to five weeks depending on salary grade and tenure) Up to 11 paid holiday opportunities Adoption assistance Sick and Safe Leave accruals of one hour for every 30 worked, up to 80 hours per calendar year unless otherwise provided by law Review our full benefits available by employment status here .
