### Job description

We are seeking a Senior Machine Learning Engineer / Applied Research Scientist to build and scale AI/ML systems for complex healthcare utilization management problems. This is a hands-on senior IC role requiring deep technical expertise, scientific rigor, healthcare domain knowledge, and ownership from problem definition through production and measurable business outcomes. Responsibilities: Own ML problems end-to-end : problem formulation, data analysis, experimentation, modeling, validation, deployment, monitoring, and iteration. Build and optimize solutions using Generative AI, LLMs, deep learning, classical ML, NLP, RAG, and retrieval technologies . Train and fine-tune models, diagnose model behavior and failure modes, and debug issues across data, model, inference, retrieval, and production systems . Design statistically rigorous experiments, establish baselines and success metrics, perform validation/error analysis, and draw defensible conclusions from quantitative and qualitative evidence. Build scalable production ML systems , identifying and resolving bottlenecks across quality, latency, cost, reliability, and infrastructure. Perform large-scale data analysis using Python, SQL, and appropriate data/ML tooling , including building custom solutions where existing tools are insufficient. Apply strong knowledge of healthcare utilization management , clinical workflows, medical necessity, authorization processes, and healthcare data. Partner with product, engineering, clinical, and business stakeholders to translate ambiguous problems into scalable technical solutions and measurable outcomes. Operate with high agency , making sound technical decisions under uncertainty and driving complex initiatives from concept through stable production. Qualifications: BS in Computer Science, ML/AI, Statistics, Engineering, or a related quantitative field ; MS with ML/AI specialization preferred . Significant hands-on experience building and operating ML/AI systems at production scale . Strong expertise in Python, SQL, ML/deep learning, LLMs/GenAI, NLP, RAG, experimentation, and distributed systems . Demonstrated ability to train/fine-tune models, debug model behavior, analyze large datasets, and design rigorous experiments and evaluations . Strong healthcare experience, preferably in utilization management or related clinical decision-support workflows . Publications in reputable peer-reviewed conferences or journals , demonstrating scientific reasoning, independent research, and technical writing capabilities. Proven ability to operate independently in ambiguous environments and deliver quantifiable clinical, operational, or business impact . What Distinguishes This Role: We are looking for someone who can operate across the full lifecycle: Healthcare problem → data → hypothesis → experiment → model → validation → scalable production system → measurable outcome This is not a research-only or prototype-focused role. The ideal candidate combines the rigor of an applied scientist , the depth of an ML engineer , and the ownership expected of a technical leader . If you will be working at home occasionally or permanently, the internet connection must be obtained through a cable broadband or fiber optic internet service provider with speeds of at least 10Mbps download/5Mbps upload. For this position, we anticipate offering an annual salary of 131,200 - 218,600 USD / yearly, depending on relevant factors, including experience and geographic location. This role is also anticipated to be eligible to participate in an annual bonus plan. At The Cigna Group, you’ll enjoy a comprehensive range of benefits, with a focus on supporting your whole health. ### Job description

We are seeking a Senior Scientist to advance quantitative bioimaging capabilities in support of immunology discovery research. In this role, you will work within a cross-functional team with access to multiple microscopy modalities, complex in vitro models, multimodal biological datasets, and advanced computational infrastructure. You will develop and apply quantitative imaging, computer-vision, and machine-learning approaches to characterize cellular and tissue phenotypes, support the development of biological models, including co-cultures, organ-on-chip systems, organoids, and tissue explants, and generate insights that advance immunology discovery programs. This role combines hands-on delivery of robust, reproducible image-analysis workflows with the evaluation and application of advanced AI/ML approaches, including biomedical image segmentation, representation learning, foundation models, and scalable computational workflows. You will partner closely with wet-lab scientists, imaging specialists, computational scientists, and other subject-matter experts to translate biological questions into rigorous and impactful analysis strategies. The role sits at the intersection of biology, bioengineering, microscopy, and computational science. Candidates with combined expertise in two or more of these areas are especially encouraged to apply. Key Responsibilities Develop, optimize, and apply imaging assays and quantitative image-analysis workflows for 2D and 3D cellular and tissue models to characterize cellular and other complex biological phenotypes. Collaborate with wet-lab assay scientists, imaging specialists, and computational scientists from experimental and assay design through image acquisition, analysis, validation, and data delivery. Extract biologically meaningful, multiparametric features from high-content imaging datasets and use them to characterize disease-relevant phenotypes, mechanisms, and treatment responses. Apply statistical, machine-learning, and deep-learning methods to identify candidate imaging biomarkers and relate imaging phenotypes to molecular, functional, and other biological readouts. Contribute to the integration of imaging data with multimodal datasets, including molecular, single-cell, spatial, or perturbational data, where appropriate. Establish quality-control and evaluation frameworks to assess image and assay quality, distinguish biological signal from technical artifacts, and define data-quality criteria, acceptance thresholds, and analytical limitations. Maintain clear experimental and analytical records; analyze, visualize, and present results rigorously to both technical and nontechnical audiences. Communicate methods, assumptions, results, uncertainties, and limitations effectively to cross-functional project teams. Stay current with advances in quantitative bioimaging, image analysis, computer vision, and AI/ML for biological imaging, and evaluate opportunities to incorporate relevant approaches into team workflows. Basic Qualifications BS, MS, or PhD in engineering, biophysics, computational biology, imaging science, bioinformatics, or a related discipline, with typically 10-12+ (BS), 8-10+ (MS), or 0-4+ (PhD) years experience. Demonstrated experience developing quantitative image-analysis or computational solutions involving one or more of the following: image segmentation, feature extraction, phenotypic profiling, object classification, or analysis of high-content imaging data. Experience with at least one relevant imaging modality or assay type, such as confocal microscopy, high-content imaging, or cell painting. Ability to assess image quality, recognize common imaging artifacts, and interpret biologically meaningful phenotypes. Working knowledge of machine-learning or deep-learning approaches for biological imaging, with the ability to understand model behavior, assess performance, and contribute to appropriate evaluation strategies. Proficiency with the Python data-science ecosystem, including NumPy, pandas, Polars, scikit-learn, and/or PyTorch. Strong biological grounding, attention to experimental rigor, and demonstrated ability to collaborate effectively with wet-lab and computational colleagues. Excellent written, verbal, and presentation skills, including the ability to explain methods, results, assumptions, and limitations to diverse audiences. Preferred Qualifications Experience working with complex in vitro models, such as organoids, organ-on-chip systems, co-cultures, tissue explants, or other advanced in vitro platforms. Experience with classical bioimage-analysis tools such as CellProfiler, scikit-image, ImageJ/Fiji, or comparable platforms. Exposure to deep-learning, self-supervised learning, foundation-model, embedding-based, or multimodal AI approaches for biological imaging. Peer-reviewed publications, patents, conference presentations, or other demonstrated scientific contributions in quantitative bioimaging, computer vision, computational biology, bioengineering, or a related field. Position will be filled at level commensurate with extent of education, experience, and accomplishment— Senior Scientist I or Senior Scientist II ​Applicable only to applicants applying to a position in any location with pay disclosure requirements under state or local law: ​ The compensation range described below is the range of possible base pay compensation that the Company believes in good faith it will pay for this role at the time of this posting based on the job grade for this position. Individual compensation paid within this range will depend on many factors including geographic location, and we may ultimately pay more or less than the posted range. This range may be modified in the future. ​ We offer a comprehensive package of benefits including paid time off (vacation, holidays, sick), medical/dental/vision insurance and 401(k) to eligible employees.​ This job is eligible to participate in our short-term incentive program. ​ This job may be eligible to participate in our long-term incentive program. Note: No amount of pay is considered to be wages or compensation until such amount is earned, vested, and determinable. The amount and availability of any bonus, commission, incentive, benefits, or any other form of compensation and benefits that are allocable to a particular employee remains in the Company's sole and absolute discretion unless and until paid and may be modified at the Company’s sole and absolute discretion, consistent with applicable law. ​ AbbVie

### Job description

The Role Moderna is seeking a computational scientist to develop artificial intelligence, machine-learning, deep-learning, and optimization methods for therapeutic mRNA design. This is a hands-on research role centered on translating biological questions into computational problems, developing and rigorously evaluating new algorithms, and turning those methods into reusable scientific software. The successful candidate will develop predictive and generative models that connect mRNA sequence and structure with experimentally measured properties. The ideal candidate combines depth in AI/ML and algorithm development with the ability to reason about biological sequence and structure. Direct RNA or mRNA experience is highly valued, but we also welcome strong computational method developers from adjacent areas of biology who can demonstrate how their expertise transfers to RNA design. Here’s What You’ll Do Formulate mRNA design challenges as well-defined machine-learning and optimization problems, including appropriate objectives, constraints, datasets, and evaluation strategies. Develop and benchmark predictive, generative, and optimization methods for biological sequence and structure. Integrate mRNA sequence, predicted or experimentally measured structure, and high-throughput assay data into computational models. Work closely with RNA biologists and experimental teams to design informative experiments, interpret results, and iteratively improve models. Evaluate new approaches against meaningful baselines and determine where additional methodological complexity is—or is not—justified. Build maintainable research software with clear documentation, testing, version control, and reproducible workflows. Scale computational methods to large datasets using cloud or high-performance computing environments. Communicate findings, limitations, and recommendations clearly to scientific and technical collaborators. Here’s What You’ll Bring to the Table (Basic Qualifications) PhD in computer science, machine learning, computational biology, bioinformatics, statistics, bioengineering, physics, or a related quantitative field with 2+ years of experience. Demonstrated experience developing and implementing machine-learning, optimization, probabilistic, or other computational methods for biological or molecular data. Strong Python programming skills and experience with modern scientific-computing or machine-learning frameworks. Experience working with biological sequence, structure, or another form of molecular data. Evidence of rigorous model evaluation, including appropriate baselines, validation strategies, and interpretation of model limitations. Ability to translate an open-ended scientific question into a tractable computational research plan. Here’s What You’ll Bring to the Table (Preferred Qualifications) Experience with RNA or mRNA biology, design, engineering, or therapeutics. Experience with generative models, transformers, graph neural networks, representation learning, active learning, Bayesian optimization, or multi-objective optimization. Familiarity with RNA secondary structure, ensemble properties, or chemical-probing data such as SHAPE. Experience integrating high-throughput experimental or NGS-derived measurements with computational models. Experience developing reusable scientific software rather than one-off analysis scripts. Experience with cloud or distributed computing environments. Publications, patents, or open-source work demonstrating a substantive contribution to algorithm or method development. Ability to work effectively across computational and experimental teams. Our Working Model As we build our company, we have always believed that an in-person culture is critical to our success. This role is 100% onsite, reflecting the importance of being physically present to support the work, collaborate closely with colleagues, and contribute to our site-based operations. Working onsite enables real-time collaboration, strong team connections, direct mentorship, and the hands-on partnership needed to deliver our mission. Join us in shaping a world where every interaction is an opportunity to learn, contribute, and make a meaningful impact. Moderna is a smoke-free, alcohol-free, and drug-free work environment. Pay & Benefits At Moderna, we believe that when you feel your best, you can do your best work. That’s why our benefits and well-being resources are designed to support you—at work, at home, and everywhere in between. Competitive healthcare, plus voluntary benefit programs to support your unique needs A holistic approach to well-being, with access to fitness, mindfulness, and mental health support Family planning benefits, including fertility, adoption, and surrogacy support Generous paid time off, including vacation, volunteer days, sabbatical, global recharge days, and a discretionary year-end shutdown Savings and investments to help you plan for the future Location-specific perks and extras The salary range for this role is $145,900.00 - $234,200.00. About Moderna Since our founding in 2010, we have aspired to build the leading mRNA technology platform, the infrastructure to reimagine how medicines are created and delivered, and a world-class team. We believe in giving our people a platform to change medicine and an opportunity to change the world. By living our mission, values, and mindsets every day, our people are the driving force behind our scientific progress and our culture. Together, we are creating a culture of belonging and building an organization that cares deeply for our patients, our employees, the environment, and our communities. LOCATION : On-Site in Boulder, CO (Foresight Dx business unit, a Natera company) &nbsp; ABOUT OUR COMPANY: Foresight Diagnostics is a molecular diagnostics company pioneering non-invasive cancer detection through highly sensitive, cell-free DNA-based liquid biopsy technologies. Our proprietary methods - originally developed at Stanford University - are designed to detect smaller tumors earlier and enable more personalized treatment strategies for patients with cancer.&nbsp; We sit at the intersection of molecular biology, bioinformatics, and next-generation sequencing (NGS), and we translate rigorous science into clinically meaningful solutions. Following our acquisition by Natera, we now combine the agility of a mission-driven startup with the scale and resources of a global leader in cell-free DNA testing. It’s an exciting moment to join us.&nbsp; Headquartered in Boulder, Colorado, our team operates in a high accountability, collaborative environment where quality and continuous improvement aren’t just values - they’re how we work every day. &nbsp; ABOUT THE ROLE: Every liquid biopsy result starts at the bench, and this role puts you right there. As part of our Boulder R&amp;D team, you’ll work hands-on with scientists developing ultrasensitive, cell-free DNA technology designed to detect smaller tumors earlier and enable more personalized treatment for patients with cancer. You’ll run experiments across the NGS workflow, from gDNA isolation and library prep to hybrid capture and sequencing, using automated liquid handlers and instruments like the TapeStation and Qubit. Along the way, you’ll learn to troubleshoot, keep instruments running smoothly, and understand why an experiment behaves the way it does, not just how to run it. You won’t work in a silo. You’ll partner daily with Associates and Scientists, summarize and present your data to the team, and write SOPs that shape how we work. Your notebooks, results, and attention to detail will be trusted and relied on by the people around you. Foresight’s Stanford-born science now has the backing of Natera, a global leader in cell-free DNA testing, so you’ll get the agility of a mission-driven startup with the resources and reach of an established company. If you have a few years of molecular biology experience and want to build deep expertise in one of the most exciting areas of modern diagnostics, in a collaborative, high-accountability culture where quality and continuous improvement are part of everyday work, we’d love to meet you. &nbsp; PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES: You will work primarily with R&amp;D assisting Associates/Scientists in various lab duties. This role will have occasional access to PHI both in paper and electronic form and have occasional access to various technologies to access PHI (paper and electronic) in order to perform the job. Adhere to environmental, health and safety (EHS) guidelines and good laboratory practices (GLP) Perform other tasks as specified by the manager/director &nbsp; QUALIFICATIONS: BS/BA in a biological science or equivalent Minimum of 2 years Molecular Biology laboratory experience required &nbsp; KNOWLEDGE, SKILLS, AND ABILITIES: gDNA isolation from biological samples (preferred). Experience with library preparation for next generation sequencing and sequencing platforms&nbsp; Experience with hybrid capture technologies Experience with laboratory automated liquid handler platforms Sample analysis/QC on the Agilent Tapestation, Nanodrop, Qubit, electrophoresis Must have proven interpersonal, communication, computer, and technical skills. Excellent oral and written communication skills required. Microsoft Excel experience required Compensation &amp; Total Rewards&nbsp; This range reflects a good-faith estimate of the base pay we reasonably expect to offer at the time of hire. Final compensation will vary based on experience, qualifications, and internal equity considerations. Comprehensive health benefits (medical, dental, vision) 401(k) with company match Generous paid time off and company holidays Additional wellness and work-life benefits Compensation Range &nbsp; $74,800.00 - $89,000.00 OUR OPPORTUNITY Natera™ is a global leader in cell-free DNA (cfDNA) testing, dedicated to oncology, women’s health, and organ health. Our aim is to make personalized genetic testing and diagnostics part of the standard of care to protect health and enable earlier and more targeted interventions that lead to longer, healthier lives. The Natera team consists of highly dedicated statisticians, geneticists, doctors, laboratory scientists, business professionals, software engineers and many other professionals from world-class institutions, who care deeply for our work and each other. When you join Natera, you’ll work hard and grow quickly. Working alongside the elite of the industry, you’ll be stretched and challenged, and take pride in being part of a company that is changing the landscape of genetic disease management. Join us in pioneering breakthroughs in healthcare. For everyone. Everywhere. Sustainably. Our inspiring and caring environment forms a global community that celebrates diversity and individuality. We encourage you to step beyond your comfort zone, offering resources and flexibility to foster your professional and personal growth, all while valuing your unique contributions. As Senior Research Scientist in the Optimization Algorithms team in Helsinki, you will contribute to the development of high-performance algorithms used by clinicians around the world to plan advanced radiotherapy treatments. This position offers the opportunity to solve challenging problems at the intersection of medical physics, mathematics, optimization, and software engineering while contributing to products that help improve cancer treatment. We are looking for someone with experience in algorithm development and numerical methods who can quickly take ownership of complex technical challenges. In addition to technical expertise, we value strong collaboration skills. We recognize that talent comes from many different paths and encourage applicants to apply even if their experience does not align perfectly with every qualification listed. This opportunity could be for you if you: Enjoy taking ownership and delivering results. Have a collaborative and innovative problem-solving mindset. Enjoy working closely with colleagues and sharing knowledge across disciplines. Your role: Develop and improve radiotherapy treatment planning algorithms. Research, design, implement, and evaluate new optimization methods and algorithmic solutions. Contribute to the full software development lifecycle, from concept and design to implementation, testing, and delivery. Collaborate closely with software engineers, physicists, researchers, product managers, and clinical users. Support successful product releases by collaborating with teams and contributing to development activities as needed. Participate in technical discussions, innovation initiatives, and the continuous improvement of our treatment planning solutions. Contribute to technical decision-making and help shape the future direction of our solutions. Experience and Qualifications: MSc or PhD in Computer Science, Mathematics, Physics, Engineering, or a related technical field. Strong programming skills in modern C++. Solid theoretical and practical experience in algorithm design, numerical methods, optimization, scientific computing, or related areas. Excellent problem-solving and analytical skills. Fluency in English language, both written and spoken. Beneficial Skills & Experience: Medical imaging, radiotherapy, medical device software, or other regulated software environments. CUDA / GPU programming. Machine learning. Quick prototyping and scripting languages such as Python. Scientific research and publication activities. What We Can Offer: Inspiring work atmosphere and a supportive work community. Meaningful work that contributes to improving cancer treatment. Opportunities for continuous learning and professional growth. International and cross-cultural working environment. Hybrid working model. Office located in the heart of Helsinki. #LI-HYBRID Who we are : We are a team of more than 72,000 highly dedicated Healthineers in more than 70 countries. As a leader in medical technology, we constantly push the boundaries to create better outcomes and experiences for patients, no matter where they live or what health issues they are facing. Our portfolio is crucial for clinical decision-making and treatment pathways. How we work : When you join Siemens Healthineers, you become one in a global team of scientists, clinicians, developers, researchers, professionals, and skilled specialists, who believe in each individual’s potential to contribute with diverse ideas. We are from different backgrounds, cultures, religions, political and/or sexual orientations, and work together, to fight the world’s most threatening diseases and enable access to care, united by one purpose: to pioneer breakthroughs in healthcare. For everyone. Everywhere. Sustainably. To find out more about Healthineers’ specific businesses, please visit our company page here . As an

Join Humana's Pharmacy Analytics and Consulting organization and the Visual Insights team as a Senior Analytics Solutions Engineer! In this role, you will help design, build, and maintain standardized reporting and analytics solutions that support ongoing program operations and business decision-making. You will work across Azure Databricks and Power BI to transform complex healthcare data into scalable reporting products, reusable data models, and consistent business metrics. This is more than a dashboard development role. You will work at the intersection of analytics engineering, data visualization, semantic modeling, and AI-enabled analytics, helping build the analytical foundation behind our reporting products while exploring new ways to make trusted data and insights more accessible through capabilities such as Genie One. As a Senior Analytics Solutions Engineer within Pharmacy Analytics and Consulting, you will: • Design, develop, and maintain standardized reporting and analytics solutions supporting the CMS GLP-1 Bridge demonstration and future related programs. • Build analytics-ready data solutions using Azure Databricks and SQL, including data transformation, modeling, validation, and reusable business logic. • Develop and maintain Power BI reports, semantic models, measures, and visualizations that provide reliable and consistent views of program performance and operational activity. • Create reusable datasets, metrics, and reporting patterns that standardize recurring analytical needs and reduce reliance on manual and ad hoc reporting. • Partner with business, clinical, pharmacy, analytics, and technology stakeholders to translate complex and evolving requirements into scalable analytical solutions. • Apply data quality, governance, documentation, and visualization practices that make analytical products reliable, understandable, and maintainable. • Explore and apply AI-enabled and conversational analytics, including capabilities such as Genie One, to improve how users interact with standardized and governed data. • Contribute to evolving team capabilities across Power BI, Azure Databricks, semantic modeling, analytics engineering, and AI. • Exercise considerable independence in determining technical approaches and solutions for moderately complex to complex assignments. analytics, AI agents, or similar AI-enabled analytics capabilities • Experience with healthcare or managed care analytics, including Medicare Part D, pharmacy claims, PBM, CMS demonstrations, or regulatory reporting • Experience with data governance practices or technologies, Microsoft analytics tools, and/or relevant Microsoft or Databricks certifications Additional Information We recognize that great candidates bring different combinations of experience. Experience developing dashboards and reporting solutions in Power BI, Tableau, or a similar visualization tool. Strong critical-thinking, problem-solving, stakeholder-management, and data-storytelling skills. Preferred Qualifications Proficiency in Python, R, or Scala for data preparation, statistical analysis, automation, or modeling. Experience with statistical modeling, machine learning, natural-language processing, predictive analytics, or experimental design. Knowledge of contact-center data, omnichannel customer journeys, Customer Service operations, and CX measures such as sentiment, effort, satisfaction, and first-contact resolution. Experience quantifying the value or return on investment generated by analytics-led improvements. ### Job description

Kensho is S&P Global’s hub for AI innovation and transformation. With expertise in machine learning, natural language processing, and data discovery, we develop and deploy novel solutions to innovate and drive progress at S&P Global and its customers worldwide. Kensho's solutions and research focus on business and financial generative AI applications, agents, data retrieval APIs, data extraction, and much more. At Kensho, we hire talented people and give them the autonomy and support needed to build amazing technology and products. We collaborate using our teammates' diverse perspectives to solve hard problems. Our communication with one another is open, honest, and efficient. We dedicate time and resources to explore new ideas, but always rooted in engineering best practices. As a result, we can innovate rapidly to produce technology that is scalable, robust, and useful. Kensho is S&P Global’s hub for AI innovation and transformation. With expertise in machine learning, natural language processing, and data discovery, we develop and deploy novel solutions to innovate and drive progress at S&P Global and its customers worldwide. Kensho's solutions and research focus on business and financial generative AI applications, agents, data retrieval APIs, data extraction, and much more. The Document Intelligence Team at Kensho is focused on designing and deploying production-grade machine learning systems that power our document processing pipelines. The team researches and implements cutting edge technologies in the document understanding domain. In our work, we emphasize scalable solutions and robust engineering. We also present and publish some of our research in the leading document intelligence conferences and journals. Our mission is to make complex visually rich documents machine-readable. Our products power both traditional document processing pipelines and the next generation LLM-driven agentic systems. Kensho’s document processing capabilities deliver highly accurate results with the best in class efficiency. We are seeking a mid-level Machine Learning Engineer to help develop and scale document understanding systems across the company. This is a hands-on, full-lifecycle ML role with a strong emphasis on PDF and image processing, high code quality, and system-level thinking. Kensho states that the anticipated base salary range for the position is $140,000 - $180,000 USD. In addition, this role is eligible for an annual incentive bonus and equity plans. At Kensho, it is not typical for an individual to be hired at or near the top of the range for their role and compensation decisions are dependent on the facts and circumstances of each case. What You’ll Do: Design and implement document processing systems that transform visually rich documents into machine readable forms Build and optimize production-level systems that process billions of document pages a year Develop pragmatic cutting edge solutions for diverse business workflows Investigate and solve challenges in document understanding Work closely with Product and Design teams to build ML-based solutions that enhance user experiences and meet business objectives Collaborate closely with the ML Operations team to create automated solutions for managing the entire ML systems lifecycle, from initial technical design to seamless implementation Who You'll Need: Bachelor's degree or higher in Computer Science, Engineering, or a related field 3+ years of significant, hands-on industry experience with machine learning (ML), computer vision (CV), natural language processing (NLP), and information retrieval systems, including designing, shipping, and maintaining production systems Strong programming skills in Python, with a working knowledge of data processing tools and ML frameworks such as PyTorch, Transformers, and HuggingFace Experience working with computer vision models such as ViT and DETR. Proven experience building ML pipelines for data processing, training, inference, maintenance, evaluation, versioning, and experimentation Demonstrated effective coding, documentation, collaboration, and communication habits Strong problem-solving skills and a proactive approach to addressing challenges Ability to adapt to a fast-paced and dynamic work environment Technologies We Love: ML: PyTorch, Transformers, HuggingFace, LangChain Tools/Toolkits: Claude Code, Git, Weights & Biases, DVC, Flask, Django, FastAPI, LiteLLM Techniques: CV (computer vision), NLP (natural language processing), Transformers, Graph Models, DETR Deployment: Airflow, Docker, Kubernetes, Jenkins, AWS, Github Action At Kensho, we pride ourselves on providing top-of-market benefits, including: Medical, Dental, and Vision insurance 100% company paid premiums Unlimited Paid Time Off 26 weeks of 100% paid Parental Leave (paternity and maternity) 401(k) plan with 6% employer matching Generous company matching on donations to non-profit charities Up to $20,000 tuition assistance toward degree programs, plus up to $4,000/year for ongoing professional education such as industry conferences Plentiful snacks, drinks, and regularly catered lunches Dog-friendly office (CAM office) Bike sharing program memberships Compassion leave and elder care leave Mentoring and additional learning opportunities Opportunity to expand professional network and participate in conferences and events Recruitment Fraud Alert: If you receive an email from a spglobalind.com domain or any other regionally based domains, it is a scam and should be reported to reportfraud@spglobal.com . S&P Global never requires any candidate to pay money for job applications, interviews, offer letters, “pre-employment training” or for equipment/delivery of equipment. Your expertise in digital data and analytical thought leadership will be used to help define OKRs, develop and measure A/B tests, scope strategic analytical questions and create analytical plans providing insights to drive outcomes for Chase’s 80+ million digital customers. You will be responsible for delivering actionable analysis in clear and compelling terms while building relationships with Digital Product, Tech, Design, Strategy and more. The ideal candidate should be self-driven and independent, motivated to establish and grow a platform to push the business forward. Experience with analytical tools such as Python, R, Alteryx, Tableau, and Adobe Analytics Experience working with Big Data environments (i.e., Snowflake, AWS) Experience with A/B testing   Chase is a leading financial services firm, helping nearly half of America’s households and small businesses achieve their financial goals through a broad range of financial products. Our mission is to create engaged, lifelong relationships and put our customers at the heart of everything we do. We also help small businesses, nonprofits and cities grow, delivering solutions to solve all their financial needs. As an Applied AI/ML Senior Associate within the Commercial & Investment Bank technology team, you will build and fine-tune language models (including LLMs) that detect and categorize complaints and sentiment across client interactions (calls, chats, emails, surveys). You’ll develop complaint taxonomies and multi-label classifiers, build training/validation/monitoring pipelines, deploy models with MLOps/data engineering partners, and ensure solutions meet privacy, regulatory, and model-risk standards. Job responsibilities • Build and fine-tune ML/NLP models (including LLMs) to detect and categorize complaints and sentiment within client interactions (calls, chats, emails, surveys) and develop taxonomies and multi-label classification systems for complaint types, severity, and root cause • Evaluate, fine-tune, and deploy pre-trained language models; conduct prompt engineering and model evaluation as needed along with build data pipelines for training, validation, and continuous model monitoring • Analyze model outputs to identify drift, bias, or degradation, and implement retraining strategies • Ensure models meet regulatory, privacy, and model-risk governance standards • Collaborate with data engineers and MLOps teams to productionize models at scale • Present findings and model performance metrics to technical and non-technical stakeholders Required qualifications, capabilities, and skills • Bachelor's or Master's degree in Computer Science, Data Science, Machine Learning, or related field and 3+ years of experience building and deploying ML/NLP models in production • Strong Python skills and experience with ML frameworks (PyTorch, TensorFlow, Hugging Face Transformers) with hands-on experience fine-tuning or working with large language models • Experience with text classification, NER, sentiment analysis, or topic modeling • Familiarity with cloud ML platforms (AWS SageMaker, Azure ML, or similar) • Solid understanding of the ML lifecycle: data prep, training, evaluation, deployment, monitoring • Strong communication skills and ability to work cross-functionally Preferred qualifications, capabilities, and skills Familiarity with model risk management and regulatory requirements Experience with vector databases, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), or LLM fine-tuning techniques (LoRA, PEFT) Knowledge of MLOps tools (MLflow, Kubeflow, Airflow) Exposure to text classification, NER, sentiment analysis, and cloud ML platforms preferred.   JPMorganChase, one of the oldest financial institutions, offers innovative financial solutions to millions of consumers, small businesses and many of the world’s most prominent corporate, institutional and government clients under the J.P. Morgan and Chase brands. Our history spans over 200 years and today we are a leader in investment banking, consumer and small business banking, commercial banking, financial transaction processing and asset management. We offer a competitive total rewards package including base salary determined based on the role, experience, skill set and location. Those in eligible roles may receive commission-based pay and/or discretionary incentive compensation, paid in the form of cash and/or forfeitable equity, awarded in recognition of individual achievements and contributions. We also offer a range of benefits and programs to meet employee needs, based on eligibility. These benefits include comprehensive health care coverage, on-site health and wellness centers, a retirement savings plan, backup childcare, tuition reimbursement, mental health support, financial coaching and more. Additional details about total compensation and benefits will be provided during the hiring process.
